Of course those who are sincere devotees, they take direct service as an opportunity, but the illusory energy is so strong that it acts on this doctrine that familiarity breeds contempt. He was thinking I was existing on his service, instead of realizing I was giving him opportunity to do me some service." (SPL Madhusudana 28th Dec '67)

Srila Prabhupada warns that when one has the opportunity for direct service to the spiritual master that one should be very careful not to develop the faulty mentality that, "Oh, he is so dependent upon my service. What would he do without me." This all due to false pride. An advanced devotee is accepting the service on behalf of his guru and Krsna, and is not dependent at all upon the service rendered to them. They are depending upon Krsna. They accept the service for the benefit of the person who offers it.

Another thing that you should be careful about, since it contributes to familiarity, is to repeatedly and overtly manifest the same problem as a device to get the attention of your spiritual master. One very damaging result of this mentality is that it causes you to become artificially dependent upon attention from , rather than being properly dependent upon his instruction. There is a gulf of difference between the two. His attention and the potency to overcome your difficulties are automatically contained within his instructions. By not following them with firm faith, you can develop the faulty mentality that, "I must get more attention from my spiritual master". This also contributes to familiarity, (especially when you find that you can get it), and it must be avoided. Don't use as a constant sanctioning agent to accommodate your lack of surrender, but instead you should be desirous to receive instructions that you will follow. I'm not implying that I have observed this nature in you, and therefore I'm correcting it. I am simply cautioning you to be conscious of it as another potential pitfall, which can cause you to disrespect your spiritual master.

\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*

Dear Bhaktin, Please accept my blessings. All glories to Srila Prabhupada.

I've read your letter given to me today, and have noted the contents.

You asked me to help you understand why it is that when you see me your desires to satisfy me go away. It seems as though it's because you're still on a sentimental platform. Somehow you must be thinking that to really serve me and satisfy me means to serve this physical body. Of course, it's not that the services you perform for me when I'm here in are displeasing. I dutifully accept them on behalf of Srila Prabhupada and Lord Krsna. But serving the physical body of the spiritual master is very limited. How many services can be done for it? My duty goes far beyond accepting this kind of service, and therefore you must learn to make the real connection of service to me through the service you render when I'm not here. That connection is much stronger, because it's a connection on the platform of vani (instructions). When you become strong in that connection, then your sentimentality will diminish, and you'll become fixed up in knowledge.

Thank you for asking me for blessings to be greatly persistent in book distribution. I pray to Srila Prabhupada that you will take up this order very seriously and that it will become your constant meditation. To strengthen your desire for book distribution, simply go on reading Srila Prabhupada's books every day in the association of devotees, and gradually your desire will become stronger and stronger. I hope that this letter finds you healthy and happily engaged in devotional service.

I remain,

Your well-wisher, Niranjana Swami

\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*

Dear Bhakta…,

Please accept my blessings. All glories to Srila Prabhupada.

If you recall, I referred to your letter during our discussion in Moscow and answered one of the questions you asked in it then. However, since I did not answer your other questions, that is the purpose of this letter.

But before I do that, I just want to add in reference to your question about personal association, that the position you're in is not wrong. If you take shelter of the process of devotional service, remain steady in your service, read Srila Prabhupada's books, and associate with Vaisnavas, you are safe. I expect that type of behavior from my disciples. Moreover, I don't expect that my disciples must see me as their sole deliverer from material life. The shelter provided by the above-mentioned activities are all major contributions to bringing us back home, back to Godhead. But beyond that, there must be an initiating spiritual master to give us the link to the guru parampara.

So I have written in my letter to aspiring disciples that I feel the need to have a personal relationship with those who are aspiring to be my disciples. But that is so that I can get to know the disciple and his needs for being encouraged in devotional service. Your letter has already helped me a lot in this regard, and for now, nothing else is required. The personal relationship is already there, and can be intensified by remembering the instructions (or example) that I've given which have led you to accept me as your initiating spiritual master. As for my personal dealings with you, that is there from my lectures. The disciple who takes that association can nicely realize how the spiritual master is present in his instructions, and will find no impediment to his progress in devotional service.

Regarding impersonalism towards the spiritual master, Srila Prabhupada once gave a personal example of this. A disciple was helping him step over a ditch while on a walk in Mayapura, and Srila Prabhupada accepted his hand as assistance. But once he got onto the other side, he pushed his disciple's hand away, indicating that he no longer needed it. Then he told everyone present that this is what the impersonalists do towards their spiritual master. They take help from the him in the form of instructions, and then they push him away, saying that they don't need him anymore. We should always feel dependent upon the mercy and instructions of our spiritual master. Otherwise, this can lead towards such impersonal dealings.

To be empowered we must simply always act as an instrument to the order of guru and Krsna. The Lord empowers those who always remember that in the execution of their service, they are always subordinate to the will of the Lord. The order for such service comes down through disciplic succession, and the potency to execute the order is also contained within it. We must simply have the firm faith, and resolute determination to fulfill the order, and everything else will follow.

In the Bhagavad-gita Lord Krsna teaches us that a devotee must become like the ocean. Although so many rivers may enter into the ocean, the ocean does not go beyond its limit, because it is already full. In the same way, we must remain full, (in Krsna consciousness), so that despite the entrance of so many dirty desires, we will not become affected. Srila Prabhupada also uses the example in the Srimad Bhagavatam, again of the ocean. He says that if one tries to stop the waves of the river by one's effort, it is much too difficult. But when the waves of the river enter into the ocean, the stronger, more powerful, waves of the ocean push back the waves of the river. Similarly, if we try to stop the waves of material desire by our own efforts, it is much too difficult. But when our desires enter into the ocean of unlimited devotional service, these material desires are pushed back on the strength of devotional service. Therefore a devotee must fill his mind with so many plans to execute his service to guru and Krsna, so that the mind will have no room for contemplating the material desires.

I hope that this letter finds you in good health and happily engaged in Lord Caitanya's Sankirtan Mission.

Your well-wisher, Niranjana Swami

\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*

Dear…,

Please accept my blessings. All glories to Srila Prabhupada.

I have your letter (undated) and have carefully noted the contents.

You are asking many different questions about service in separation, but first I will say that, in principle, I do not mind if you come back from traveling sankirtan to attend my lectures when I am . One of my purposes for coming is to give my disciples the strength that they need to go on with their service, and I do that, primarily, through my lectures. Yes, your authorities are right by always stressing the importance of being out on book distribution. Our preaching mission is very important, especially now when the atmosphere is becoming so degraded. And you are dutifully taking up this mission. But I purposely do not give lectures during the day, so as to not disrupt our book distribution mission. That is one reason why I was explaining during one of my recent visits to , that the residents of should not be neglected in our attempts to distribute as many books as possible. When you come back from traveling sankirtan, you can go out and distribute books, all day, in . There are millions of conditioned souls in , who don't know it yet, but they are looking for Lord Caitanya's mercy. We are in the process of building a large temple just so that these people can regularly come and take the darshan of Sri Sri Gour Nitai, take Krsna prasadam, and regularly hear about Krsna from the devotees. This temple is for them, just as much as it is for Sri Sri Gour Nitai and Their devotees.

So considering that wherever you are, you must attend classes every day anyway, if you are in …attending my class, there should not be any disturbance to our book distribution mission. You and my other disciples who are distributing books should become so expert in not wasting any time, that not a moment of time is wasted by having to come to wherever I am lecturing.

However, I can easily understand how it may not always be possible for you to come back from traveling sankirtan. Sometimes you may be just too far away, and a lot of time could be wasted in traveling. Or, coming back could create so many other problems as well. During these times, you must then appropriately apply your understanding of service in separation.

You are in the process of learning what it means to be a servant of the spiritual master. My disciples should be coming to the understanding that I am not in so much need for services to this body. Whatever small amount of this type of service there is, I accept it only out of duty. But my real desire is to see you advance, and become happy in Krsna consciousness. I'm completely convinced that the mission that Srila Prabhupada has entrusted to all of us, can make all of us very happy in Krsna consciousness, and will take all of us back home, back to Godhead. Seeing you chanting, hearing, dancing, and hearing about your activities on book distribution brings me the greatest pleasure. Your remembrance of this, wherever you are, is real service in separation. In such remembrance, on the spiritual platform, there is really no such separation. We are associating together in service.

Srila Prabhupada nicely explains this point in one letter dated May, 1968:

My dear Uddhava, Please accept my blessings. I am just in receipt of your letter forwarded from New York. Please be happy in separation. I am separated from my Guru Maharaj since 1936 but I am always with him so long I work according to his direction. So we should all work together for satisfying Lord Krishna and in that way the feelings of separation will transform into transcendental bliss. Hope you are well. I am very anxious to know about the church.

If they do not agree then take back the check.

Your ever well-wisher,

A. C. Bhaktivedanta Swami

So, in summary, it is not wrong to wish personal association, so that when it's available you can take full advantage of it by hearing very attentively. But, as you become more fixed up in hearing, you will be able to continuously associate in remembrance of my instructions.

I hope that this letter meets you in good health, and happily engaged in Lord Caitanya's Sankirtan Mission.

Your well-wisher, Niranjana Swami

\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*

Dear…dasa

Please accept my blessings. All glories to Srila Prabhupada.

I tried to explain the basic principles to you last time when we discussed this matter. The point is that in Vaisnava philosophy, the principle of taking shelter or guidance from Vaisnavas other than one's initiating spiritual master is honored throughout the tradition. The duty of the spiritual master is to see that the disciple advances in Krsna consciousness. If he sees that the disciple can make further progress by hearing from another Vaisnava, then WITH HIS BLESSINGS, he may go to that Vaisnava for instruction. However, it is the duty of the disciple to bring back what he has heard that appears to be more than his own spiritual master has given him, and to reconfirm this with his own spiritual master. If it is just that you are inspired by a particular Vaisnava more than your own spiritual master, then again, with the blessings of the spiritual master, you may serve that Vaisnava, and thus get his mercy. Although the subject matter is delicate, and there are many details, I am trying my best to give you here a basic understanding, hoping that in the future I can elaborate on this further, if necessary. But in the meantime, I will give you a few relevant quotes from Krsna Bhajanamrtam by Narahari Sarkara Thakur.

Verse 48

Just as a faithful son may go out for earning money and subsequently brings to his father the wealth gained, later the son may ask for some allowance from the father and whatever he receives from the father he is entitled to spend for his own enjoyment. Similarly, a disciple may hear some instructions from another advanced Vaisnava but after gaining that good instruction he must bring it and present it to his own spiritual master. After presenting them he should hear the same teachings again from his spiritual master with appropriate instructions.

Verse 49

If the son earns money but does not give it to the father and instead, directly enjoys the wealth, he is considered to be a fallen son and a sinful person. Similarly, a disciple who listens to the words of other Vaisnavas, even if their instructions are proper and true, but does not reconfirm these teachings with his own spiritual master and instead directly personally accepts these instructions, is considered a bad disciple and a sinner.

Verse 50

For this reason, in all circumstances all Vaisnavas are offered respect like one offers respect to one's spiritual master. However, with body, mind and words one serves one's own spiritual master.

Verse 51

Even if in the performance of one's devotional activities one has disobeyed the spiritual master, still one should not give him up, but should remain faithfully with him, because all authorities say that the shelter of one's own spiritual master is best and perfect (even if another spiritual master is more powerful).

\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*

July 31, 1995

Dear…devi dasi,

Please accept my blessings. All glories to Srila Prabhupada.

Thank you very much for your letter which I received while here in Kiev. I've carefully read it and noted the contents.

First thing you should know is that I am very happy to receive letters from my disciples— especially informative letters which help me to help all of you. Devotees may say that "the guru knows everything", but this is simply not true. The guru knows everything he needs to know, because a genuine guru must know Krsna. But to know what he needs to know about his disciple, he must get help from Lord Paramatma. It's not that Paramatma is telling everything to the spiritual master about his disciples from within the spiritual master's heart. But Krsna can dictate within the spiritual master's heart and to others within their heart to bring the spiritual master what he needs to know to help his disciple.

For instance, exactly two years ago, I awoke within the middle of the night, and was thinking about one devotee in the United States with whom I had spoken several months prior. During that conversation I told him that I would agree to initiate him if he better adjusted his life for always remembering Krsna. He revealed to me then that he had terminal cancer, and wanted to be initiated before leaving his body. He wanted to go to Vrnda- vana, and therefore I agreed to meet him in Vrndavana in November of that year. However, when I awoke that night in July, I could not fall back to sleep, so I decided to write a letter to one devotee who knew this person who had cancer. The letter was received electronically within a couple of days, and the devotee wrote back a letter saying that this person with cancer (Rantide- va dasa) decided to give up all of his treatments because the treatments were not working, and because he could not get enough money to go to Vrndavana, he decided to move to the devotee farm where this other devotee was living. He told me that Ran- tideva dasa had very little time to live, and that he was setting an excellent example of the importance of thinking of Krsna at the time of leaving one's body. I did not know any of this. But Krsna arranged for me to know from this other devotee. I wrote back a letter accepting Rantideva dasa as my disciple and he left his body two days later.

This is an example of how Krsna helps the spiritual master know what he needs to know to help a sincere disciple.

So you may go on writing letters to inform me about what you are doing. I'm happy to hear that you are worshiping Sri Sri Gaur Nitai, and that you are serving Lord Caitanya's mission in so many other ways. Please go on with your sincere efforts, and certainly Lord Caitanya will also take notice of your efforts to please Him.

Hoping this meets you in good health.

Your well-wisher, Niranjana Swami

\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*

May 10, 1994

Dear…,

Please accept my blessings. All glories to Srila Prabhupada.

The last thing you said to me before I left the program on Sunday was that you were sorry. These two words of yours "I'm sorry" are used so often by you to me, that it's impossible for me to know what you're apologizing for every time you use them. Are you apologizing for your last letter? Are you apologizing for your existence? Are you apologizing for not following my instructions? That I don't know.

I'm hoping that you were apologizing for your last letter, but if you weren't then I'll address your questions within it.

I don't know anything about…'s disciples, and neither am I interested. Srila Prabhupada has given me this family of Vaisnavas to associate with— ISKCON— and I don't feel any need or desire to go outside Srila Prabhupada's family. For whatever reason, it seems that these people you've met did feel the need. Most people who leave the association of ISKCON devotees to either start new associations or connect themselves with existing associations derive their strength from criticising ISKCON and it's leadership. I do not find that method of recruitment to be a very positive contribution to Lord Caitanya's mission. If they are actually Vaisnavas then they will derive their strength from higher authorities, and they will preach with the real purity required to attract and sustain those to whom they are preaching. I do not find blatant criticism of Vaisnavas something which is very pure. Such so-called "preaching" requires a good facade to attract disgruntled associates of ISKCON such as yourself. I don't know if the people you have associated with fall into this category or not, and as I said, I'm really not interested in knowing anything about them. But it seems like you've already been attracted by your initial association. They could be creating a very nice facade for you, so I'm just trying to warn you. What are they actually doing to spread Lord Caitanya's Sankirtan mission?

Moreover, it seems that you've been told by "someone" that ISKCON leadership is materialistic. If you think that I'm materialistic, then why do you want to remain my disciple? And if I'm not materialistic, then why can't you associate with those devotees with whom I am associating? If you say that it's because they are too materialistic, then indirectly you are saying that I am materialistic since many of these devotees are following my instructions. And continuing with that line of reasoning, you would also be saying that I am lacking the necessary purity to change all these materialistically motivated devotees into pure- hearted souls. If that's your conclusion, then maybe you should look for a spiritual master who's much purer than I am. If I can't change them, then how will I ever change you? You are always finding fault with devotees.

I hope that this letter finds you Krsna conscious.

Your well-wisher, Niranjana Swami

\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*

January 23, 1994 Dear…devi dasi,

Please accept my blessings. All glories to Srila Prabhupada.

Thank you very much for your nice sweater, which I'll now wear along with the nice coat that you gave me last year. I've been wearing that coat all this winter. I immediately gave my old coat away when I got this one. It was even tested for winter weather in Russia, and passed the test with ease.

But even more than than the sweater, I thank you for the message in your card. It brings the spiritual master great happiness to know that his disciples are finding shelter in his instructions. This is real loving reciprocation. And when I think of the hardships that you've had to endure, and the fact that you're trying to strengthen this link with your spiritual master by improving your chanting, it increases my desire to see you advance in Krsna consciousness. Please go on chanting, develop your faith in the holy name, and make this your last birth in the material world.

I hope that this letter meets you in good health.

I am always, Your well-wisher,

Niranjana Swami

## Preaching and book distribution

You ask if book distribution is the only way to cure some one with an abominable consciousness. The answer is "no" it's not the only way. But for all practical purposes, it can be the most effective way. Srila Prabhupada always emphasized that if we want to cure our diseased condition, we must constantly glorify the holy name, pastimes, and instructions of the Lord. The difficulty is that we have no taste for this constant chanting. Without this spontaneous attraction for chanting, we must voluntarily place ourselves in an environment which will push us in the direction of glorifying the Lord. Book distribution has the unique quality of providing this environment. Granted, sometimes we can become ritualistic in our efforts to sell Krsna conscious literature, and it may seem that we are failing to expand upon the glories of the Lord. But because our efforts are aimed at service to the Vaisnavas, by directly assisting them in spreading the Lord's glories, ultimately we are expanding the Lord's glories. Gradually we can become cured from our diseased condition and begin to develop more and more of an attraction to speak about the Lord in every circumstance. For instance in the following quote from a letter written by Srila Prabhupada, he clearly indicates that the service rendered by those devotees who are spreading the Lord's glories is definitely recognized by Krsna, and the result is that devotion to Him grows at "double rates".

\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*

...I am happy to hear that you continued to chant despite so many doubts and skepticism. That is the process. Even if there may be doubts and skepticism, if one continues the chanting process, the doubts will all disappear, and real knowledge will be revealed by the Grace of Krishna. There is the example given of the jaundice patient. He is suffering from disease, and when given sugar-candy, which is the cure, he finds it very bitter and distasteful. But that does not mean the sugar-candy is not very sweet and delicious; it is simply due to his diseased condition that it seems bitter. In order to be cured from his disease, he must take the medicine of sugar-candy, despite the apparently bitter taste, and as he becomes cured, the real sweet taste of the candy is gradually revealed. Similarly, we are diseased, and only if we take to this chanting process may we be cured. Maya may put so many doubts and worthless arguments into our minds, but if we continue the chanting, the curing process will go on, never mind the doubts, and gradually we will get a taste of that sweet nectar of Krishna Nama Sankirtan. That is the process; and you may explain it to all your friends, so they may understand the nature of their doubts and skepticism, and be benefited. Yes, your devotion will grow at "doubled rates" as you give more and more service to the Supreme Lord. Krishna states in the Bhagavad Gita that the one who is spreading KC is the one most dear to Him. So, you are doing the most valuable service to the Lord by introducing your class-fellows and friends to this Sankirtan movement, and therefore, you will feel more and more increase in your devotion to the Lord. Please continue to do like this, and Krishna will be pleased, and you will be always happy in this life, and what to speak of the next....

\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*

From the tone of your letter it seems as though you're being tested by Krsna. I can easily understand the situation you're in, since you haven't been out on book distribution for some time. It's for that very reason that most book distributors fear taking any time off from book distribution, because they all know how much effort it takes to regulate the mind and senses in preparation for each day's encounters. I remember when I was doing full-time book distribution, there was a time when we felt that we even had to distribute on Sundays for a few hours, no matter what, just so that we wouldn't lose momentum from taking one day a week off. It's not that I'm recommending that this policy be adopted. It's important to get at least one full day for rest and extra study when performing full-time book distribution. However, in principle, a dedicated book distributor should know that any time not spent on Sankirtan should be used to increase his determination and enthusiasm for Sankirtan. That service requires that kind of resolute determination.

\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*

In answer to your questions about aggression on Sankirtan, since Srila Prabhupada has already addressed this question, I will refer to that quote here:

"Regarding the question you have raised about traveling sankirtana parties and selling books, yes, we want money. So that is the real preaching, selling books. Who can speak better than the books? At least whoever buys, he will look over. If you have to sell books, do it by hook or by crook. The real preaching is selling books. You should know the tactic how to sell without irritating. What your lecture will do for three minutes, but if he reads one page his life may be turned. We don't want to irritate anyone, however, if he goes away by your aggressive tactics, then you are nonsense and it is your failure. Neither you could sell a book, neither he would remain. But if he buys a book that is the real successful preaching. That is the certificate of my Guru Maharaja, if someone, brahmacari, would sell a one pais magazine, if one of our brahmacaris would go and sell a few copies, he would be very very glad and say "Oh, you are so nice!" So distribution of literature is our real preaching. Now if you cannot handle the matter nicely, that is your fault. But the success of your preaching will be substantiated by how many books are sold. Anything you want to sell, you have to little canvass, so he gives some money for the service of Krsna.

That is his good luck and he gets the chance to read some transcendental knowledge. But if you only irritate and he goes away, that is your less intelligence. The fact is that we have to adopt the same tactics as ordinary salesmen adopt, but the difference is we do it for the satisfaction of Krsna, they do it for sense gratification....

...So far irritation is concerned, a child is also irritated when he is given instructions, but that does not mean that we should stop. Invite them to our feast, that is a better (infication) (?)....

....What your one hour of preaching will do, they will hear and go away, but if they have one book at home they will read, their friends will read, and something solid work is done. So the art is to sell many many books and not to irritate the public,... (SPL, Bali Mardan, 30th September, 1972)

So from this quote we can understand that the best way to use our aggression is to convince them to take a book, but without irritating them. In this quote, Srila Prabhupada is balancing the point between overaggression, and being overly intimidated. Sometimes people will become disturbed, no matter how we deal with them, because they are already disturbed! Our mere presence is a threat to their sense gratification, especially when we expose how much people have exploited religious practices to justify their own sense gratification. So we should not be intimidated by their disturbance. However, if we don't provoke them by restraining from strong preaching, and by that tactic they willingly take a book, that is much better, than provoking them, and they end up walking away empty-handed. However, you must still be prepared to preach as the following quote indicates:

"Regarding book distribution, everyone, including book distributors, must follow the standard regulations. Book distribution is preaching, it should not be thought of as done for money. It is executed as a preaching purpose. With these guidelines in mind, try to distribute as many books as possible." (SPL, Ku- rusrestha, 23rd July, 1973)

\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*

I was very happy to return from the Soviet Union to find your name on the list of top distributors in every North American newsletter published since I left United States in early July. I want to tell you how much this inspires me in my preaching. By your steady endeavor to distribute Srila Prabhupada's books, I'm reminded that I must remain steady in my endeavor to encourage others to take up this most important mission. In this way, you are helping me in my service to Srila Prabhupada.

\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*

You say that you are praying to get my mercy, but you should know that I have no mercy of my own to give. Whatever good you may see in me is only due to Srila Prabhupada's mercy. Just as Prabhupada gives the analogy that the spiritual master does not say that he is distributing his own mercy. He is simply carrying the mercy that is coming from Krsna. Just as the rain cloud distributes the water everywhere without discrimination. But the rain cloud is not carrying its own water- it carries the water that has been taken from the ocean. So, in this way, the mercy is simply coming down through disciplic succession. If you take it, then your duty is to also distribute it for the welfare of others.

So I ask that you please try to catch hold of this mercy that is coming from Lord Caitanya and get the determination and enthusiasm you need to take up His Sankirtan Mission. Lord Caitanya is namo maha vadanyaya, He is the most merciful in carnation, and if you fully immerse yourself in His mission, He will certainly pour unlimited mercy down upon you.

Hoping this meets you in good health and enthusiastically absorbed in Sankirtan Yajna.

\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*

In the second letter, there were no questions, except that you asked for my forgiveness for your lack of compassion towards the people to whom you distribute Srila Prabhupada's books. And you also asked that I give you shelter. You'll find that so much shelter is already there when you remember my instructions. I've asked you to please distribute Srila Prabhupada's books, and have given so many other supporting instructions in my lectures. You should understand that those instructions are just as much directed to you as those given directly to you. And by acting upon them, you will gain the strength that you need to continue with your service in book distribution. I am very pleased by the sacrifices made by those devotees who selflessly go out to the conditioned souls to induce them to take one of Srila Prabhupada's books. When I look and see how many thousands of people, whose lives have been changed from despair to hope by reading Srila Prabhupada's books, it makes me feel so proud of Srila Prabhupada, and so fortunate to have the opportunity to serve him. We must give as many people as possible this opportunity to have Srila Prabhupada's association.

Even if you are lacking compassion, by distributing Srila Prabhupada's books, compassion will come. It is such a purifying activity that quickly you'll begin to experience all the dirty things in the heart, which are preventing you from feeling genuine compassion, going away. By serving one who is compassionate, we have hope of becoming compassionate ourselves.

\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*

Your feelings about preaching to people who are coming closer to Krsna are not unlike the feelings that all preachers experience. Some people will take to Krsna consciousness and some will not. Krsna says that those who do— are very rare. But we still must preach, because it is very pleasing to Krsna. Detached preaching means that we are not interested in the results of our preaching, but we are simply interested to try to make others Krsna conscious. If we have that genuine detachment, then Krsna will work through us, and people will become attracted. If they get pulled away by maya, then what can we do? We must depend upon Krsna for the results. But we also must make the endeavor to please Him. Therefore it is not bad to become involved in other people's lives when you are giving them Krsna. That is the mercy of a devotee. Please keep on preaching and make this attitude your meditation, and Krsna will help you.

\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*

One point that you make is that you are eager to perform a potent service, and you seem to indicate that because you only distribute, at most, five big books daily that the service you're performing is not potent enough. Why should you think that way? In one letter Srila Prabhupada says: .. ."But if he buys a book that is the real successful preaching. That is the certificate of my Guru Maharaja, if someone, brahmacari, would sell a one pais magazine, if one of our brahmacaris would go and sell a few copies, he would be very very glad and say "Oh, you are so nice!" So distribution of literature is our real preaching." ... "Real preaching" (that Srila Prabhupada refers to) means that that the activity of distributing Srila Prabhupada's books is very potent. Now you must become potent by developing the desire to please him and surrendering to the service of distributing his books. Srila Prabhupada is pleased when you make the effort. Leave the results up to Krsna. If you're attached to the results, and want to enjoy them yourself, by getting recognition, then you'll falsely renounce your duty, and will look for something else to do. But since you are looking for the most potent service, then I strongly recommend that you continue to go out and distribute books. Although you may not be getting big results, that does not matter. Results will come if you have the desire to please guru and Krsna. Recognition will also come. But the real recognition comes when we are not looking for it. In the Caitanya Caritamrta, Madhya 4.147, Srila Prabhupada writes: "When a person, out of humility, does not desire fame, people generally think him quite humble and consequently give him all kinds of fame. Actually a Vaishnava does not hanker after fame or a great reputation."

So, being recognized by guru, means that he gives his instructions so that you can act as a humble servant, and follow his order. By acting on those orders, you are being given the opportunity to realize that whatever good result manifests is by the mercy of guru and Krsna. A devotee always feels himself to be simply an instrument in disciplic succession. When you actually think in this way, you will be recognized by Krsna. What greater recognition could a devotee ever want?

\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*

I'm very pleased to hear the attitude that you have. Yes, it is your infinite duty to devote all your energies to the distribution of Srila Prabhupada's mission. In the Caitanya Caritamrta Adi [9.36](https://vedarama.com/en/books/cc-adi/9.36), Lord Caitanya is quoted:

**"Therefore I order every man within this universe to accept this Krishna consciousness movement and distribute it everywhere."**

Lord Caitanya is instructing everyone to help Him distribute the fruits of love of Godhead, and Srila Prabhupada so perfectly embodied the ideal example of one who took that instruction to the core of his heart. He also was so tolerant, more tolerant than we can even imagine. He began this movement single- handedly, and endured so many difficulties. But because he had firm faith in the order of his guru, Lord Caitanya used him as his instrument to distribute Krsna consciousness everywhere. We saw Srila Prabhupada construct such an expansive Society with such expansive vision for engaging the whole world in the Sankirtan mission, and we, as tiny and insignificant as we are, are being given the opportunity to humbly assist him. There is no greater fortune than this anywhere within the three worlds. So, if you just give this one life to Srila Prabhupada and Lord Caitanya, you will certainly receive His mercy, beyond all expectations.

\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*

Do not be too disappointed that your heart is not yet full of compassion for the conditioned souls. And neither should you be too disappointed because you still do not have the "mood" of a sankirtan devotee. Your desire to take the role of an assistant to your husband is appropriate. It is easy to become confused about one's role, since in society we must assume so many roles in our efforts to satisfy others. Naturally, our primary responsibility is to satisfy guru and Krsna, but you should not think that satisfying your husband by acting in this assistant capacity is outside the realm of service to guru and Krsna. It is proper for a chaste wife to assist her husband. As the heart becomes purified by the execution of prescribed duties in Krsna consciousness, the day will come when you will be able to feel more spontaneous in your efforts to preach and give others Krsna consciousness. Just go on with your duties and don't forget that the goal of all your endeavors is to please guru and Krsna.

\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*

You seem to have forgotten that if one wants to be an effective instrument for spreading Lord Caitanya's Sankirtan Movement, one MUST be a surrendered servant of the spiritual master.

Srila Prabhupada writes in one letter: "A Vaisnava always feels himself incapable to repay his debt to his spiritual master. Therefore he works very hard in order to try and repay that debt. He knows that without the mercy of the spiritual master one cannot preach this Krsna consciousness and therefore he always tries to act in such a way that he may please his guru."

So though your desire to preach is pleasing to me, the fact that you cannot properly control your behavior, as shown in the statements you made to me yourself, ("I have no humility at all.") ("I can't distribute books at all, I'm just trying to push the people and now I'm getting strong reactions.") is neither pleasing to your authorities,…devi dasi, nor to myself. Therefore how can you expect to preach? You must also behave properly.

Srila Prabhupada also writes, "Caitanya Mahaprabhu has taught us, apani acari prabhu jivare sikhaya. If you don't behave yourself as an ideal man, you cannot preach. Your preaching will not be successful."

And also in one lecture he states, "Preacher has to meet so many fallen souls. So he, if he becomes disturbed, then he cannot preach. Therefore dhira. This word is here: anupasyanti, tam atma-stham ye anupasyanti dhira. Without being dhira, you cannot perceive the presence of the Supreme Personality of Godhead within your heart, because the God is there in Paramatma feature. But you have to become dhira, without being disturbed."

By writing all of this I am not suggesting that I am ordering you to stop preaching forever. However, I am instructing you that if you do want to preach, you must be submissive, and must try to satisfy your spiritual master by following his instructions. Then, when Lord Caitanya is satisfied with your surrender, He will empower you with the potency to not only preach, but to preach effectively. It is not effective preaching when you leave most people that you meet with an unfavorable impression of Lord Caitanya's Sankirtan Movement. This cannot continue.

\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*

Dear devotees,

Please accept my blessings. All glories to Srila Prabhupada. This morning I was asked to give a talk to approximately 15 gurukula girls here in the United States, who were all preparing themselves to go out on the Prabhupada Marathon. Yesterday I spoke to… about 20 gurukula boys. I spoke about our book distribution in the countries of the former Soviet Union, and then showed them a video that Yamaraja Prabhu made several years ago just after the 1991 Prabhupada Marathon. I hadn't seen that video for several years, and so I watched it along with them.

After just seeing this video, it rekindled my memories of earlier days when practically every single devotee in the temple would burst out of the temple for the Prabhupada Marathon, with full enthusiasm, anxious to disseminate Srila Prabhupada's transcendental literatures for the enlightenment of human society. Nothing else mattered. It was the time for everyone to go out to the streets, subways, factories, homes, and wherever they could find conditioned souls to preach. Although it is now many years later, that same mission is still weighing heavily upon our heads. I am very pleased to hear that all of you are still feeling the weight and are nicely planning your marathon. Please know that I am very grateful to those devotees who take up this important missionary work because it is so pleasing to Srila Prabhupada. Although my blessings for your success in this endeavor may not invoke Lord Caitanya's intervention, I will still offer them because I know that I must. But I have great conviction that Srila Prabhupada's blessings for this book distribution activity is so potent, that they will continue to pour forth for many generations to come. I therefore also pray that Srila Prabhupada will pour his blessings upon all of you, and that you will all realize your goals for spreading Krsna consciousness in your prabhu datta desa.

I can say that these gurukula boys and girls are now very enlivened to go out and distribute books. Last year, this same group of girls distributed 2,000 books in a little more than a week. They now want to beat their previous year's scores. Please continue to inspire these devotees, and continue to set a nice example for them by showing them that our "life" in Krsna consciousness comes from preaching. Maybe next year we can show them some videos or photos from the 1997 Srila Prabhupada Marathon?

Param vijate sri krsna sankirtanam...

Your well-wisher, Niranjana Swami

\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*

Thank you very much for your inspiring report. While reading the description of your participation in the Religious Unity Meeting, and the outcome of your participation, I started to read it out loud to relish it even more. We must take every available opportunity to show others what Krsna conscious devotees are like. Nothing can surpass our philosophy. The more exposure people get to exemplary devotees, the more all these false misconceptions about devotees will be driven out of their minds. Any intelligent person cannot help but appreciate Vaisnava qualities. Please go on preaching purely and no doubt you will bring great pleasure to your spiritual master.

I am also happy to hear that you are taking my previous message seriously, and you are also recognizing the need to improve the quality of our preaching and "training". If they absorb their minds in Srila Prabhupada's books and great care and attention is given to them to help them understand how to apply what they read, then these Nama Hatta devotees can become a dynamic preaching force in due course of time as well. It is not necessary for everyone to give up their positions in life to move into a temple. Let them purify themselves by executing their grhasta duties, and at the same time, they must increase their hearing and chanting about Krsna. With regular association they will become strong.

However, please remember always that when you are preaching in…or in areas where devotees can easily visit these temples, you must always encourage these devotees to take part in the temple events. As much as possible, we should avoid any sectarian mentality.

\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*

Thank you very much for sending me the results of your Sri Nrsmhadeva Marathon. It was very enlivening to see the quantity of books that were distributed by the Donetsk devotees. However, I must be honest, and I must tell you that what was even more inspiring was to see the number of devotees that took part. The Lord is very much inclined to bestow benedictions when He sees His devotees united in devotional service. Big distributor, little distributor these designations are not relevant. Let everyone try their best to render some service to Lord Caitanya's Sankirtan Mission in a cooperative way. Our real designation is nitya krsna dasa. Everyone has their part to play. And if the proper consciousness is there, that we want to work cooperatively to please guru and Krsna, then certainly everyone will make nice advancement in Krsna consciousness.

\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*

Thank you very much for your report. I am happy to hear that you are preaching successfully to the congregation in... Congregational preaching requires a deep vision. The congregation must be educated to not only accept a spiritual master, but they must know just what kind of a commitment is required to accept a spiritual master. The congregational preacher should not think that his business is finished simply by bringing congregational devotees to the point of seeking initiation, or even to the point of receiving initiation, and then he leaves all the rest of the responsibility for elevating the congregation in the hands of their spiritual masters. The congregational preacher must become a spiritual leader himself, and he should feel the weight of the responsibility to continue to encourage initiated congregational members so that they do not go astray after taking initiation. If he does not feel confident about their integrity, then he should not recommend them for initiation. Not all congregational members will be initiated. Many should remain just congregational members until they are able to make the commitment to become an initiated disciple. And if the congregational preacher is inspired and responsible, he will provide the support that the congregation requires to remain strong and dedicated congregational members, whether they are initiated or not. To do this, one must become fixed up in the philosophy contained in Srila Prabhupada's books, and he should teach it to others.

\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*

There are different ways to preach in colleges and institutes in the West. One is to get formal classroom lectures by getting invited to lecture on various topics by the professor. Generally we are invited by professors to speak on a very wide area of topics, such as philosophy, religion, comparative religion, sociology, psychology, health education, etc., etc. We generally find that in the West, once a particular professor invites a devotee to speak to their class, they will invite devotees back every semester. The important thing is that the presentation must be authoritative, and must be presented in a way that helps the students gain a better understanding of the sociological, religious, and philosophical aspects of our movement, without preaching too heavily. Generally, the students will very much appreciate such a talk and many of them will come up after the presentation to ask further questions and even to purchase some books.

Another way of preaching in the institutes is by forming clubs, such as vegetarian clubs, or philosophy clubs. But these clubs must be formed by the students themselves. In institutions in the West, the students have the right to form a club of their own interest, they are given a room to meet in, and are allowed to have their own expense account with funds that they collect amongst themselves. We, as preachers of Krsna consciousness do not have the right to start a club in a school, but if we can find enough students who are interested, then they can gather other interested students. If there are enough interested students, then they can start the club themselves and then they can invite us to either come and speak, or to teach vegetarian cooking. Generally, most clubs in the West are started with signatures of approximately 10 interested students. If there are students coming to the temple, you can ask them to inquire at their schools just what kind of facilities the school allows for this kind of extracurricular activity.

Another nice way to preach in schools is simply by going outside the school with magazines, invitations, and some simple prasadam, and just hand out invitations to the students, speaking with any interested students you meet. There is a devotee I know in the West, a disciple of Hrdayananda Maharaja. His name is Sarvatma dasa, who is very expert at this. He just makes friends with the students, and then invites them back to the flat that he rents as a small preaching center one by one. He gives them nice prasadam, and just makes friends with them. Then he organizes a program, invites all the students he's met over the past month, and invites a guest speaker (generally a sannyasi) to come and speak. He cooks a very nice feast, and sometimes he'll get as many as 60-70 people to attend the program— just from the people he meets on campus. He's very expert. There is an E-mail address where this devotee can be reached. It's not his E-mail address, but I believe that someone will pass a message on to him.

These are just some ideas for you to think about. There are many ways we can preach to college students, if we are simply convinced that college preaching is very important and we have a strong desire.

\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*

In principle, I am pretty dead set against the way many devotees attempt to employ modern music in the name of preaching. Granted, there may be a few exceptions, but for the most part, it is of very little preaching value. That said, when even those who are exceptional (for preaching, that is), perform for the "pleasure" of the devotees and not really for preaching, I also consider such a "performance" of very little value. Maybe I'm too conservative, but that's just the way that I am. I derive no gratification (material or spiritual) from rock and roll music.

Other than this, I am most inspired by your efforts to continue the training programs for the Nama Hatta attendees. As mentioned previously, I am convinced that steady programs such as these will bear a very positive fruit for our future in …

Although many of these people will not live in temples, if they can gain a firm philosophical foundation for Krsna consciousness, they will become good examples for society while integrating their lives within society as devotees of Krsna. But even more than that, as they grow older, their children grow up, and their social obligations diminish, they will hopefully become ready to more actively preach, by following the nice example that you are showing yourself. We want Krsna consciousness to take a firm root within modem society, and the more Krsna consciousness is there within our members, the deeper the roots will grow.

\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*

Recently we were speaking about one maidservant of Srivasa Pandit whose name had formerly been Dukhi (sad). Everyday she was going to the Ganges, filling up buckets, one by one, to bring for Lord Caitanya to take His bath after long kirtans. One day, Lord Caitanya simply noticed the buckets lined up, one after another, outside the house of Srivasa Pandit. He asked, "Who is bringing these buckets of water every day?" The devotees replied that these buckets were being brought by Srivasa Pandit's maidservant named "Dukhi". Lord Caitanya replied that, "She may be named Dukhi, but within my heart she is always "Sukhi" (happy). Lord Caitanya thus blessed her to be eternally happy in devotional service. Vrndavana dasa Thakur thus went on to describe that simply by shaving one's head, there is no guarantee that one will escape the punishment of Yamaraja. The example of "Sukhi" serves to show us that our steady service rendered for the Lord's pleasure will capture His attention, and all blessings for eternal devotional service will come.

Similarly, by your steady efforts to enlighten the people of…certainly take notice. Dukhi was not standing out in front, trying to capture the Lord's attention with some big display of service. She was simply meditating on the her service to Him, and was dutifully performing it day after day. The few buckets of water in the same place every day is what captured the Lord's attention. Similarly, do not think that "big book scores" are the necessary requirements for satisfying the Lord. Just go out, and make your effort to turn others' attention towards Krsna. Even if only a few books go out, because the effort is there every day, Recently we were speaking about one maidservant of with transcendental knowledge, Lord Caitanya will Lord Caitanya will certainly see that more than anything else.

Thank you very much for your sincere service. I am always indebted to my disciples who are eager to accept so much personal sacrifice to serve Srila Prabhupada's mission.

\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*

Yes. I am planning to fly to India on October 1st. I hope to stay in Vrndavana for one month, and then I will return to the United States for one more visit this year.

I just now returned from a wonderful program here in Voronezh, Russia. There is no temple here. Just a small Nama Hatta. It is only the second time I visited this city. When we went to the rented hall to give the lecture, it turned out that the hall was in the same building as a discotheque, and the music was blaring much too loudly. So the devotees found a small soccer field and stadium nearby. We sat down right in the corner of the field in front of the stadium, had a kirtan, and I gave a BG lecture to about 50 people. By the end of the lecture, another 20 people had gathered, listened to the end of the lecture, and then stayed to watch and even some took part in another kirtan. The devotees liked the lecture so much that they all started applauding after it was over. After that, everybody took prasadam in great ecstasy. We finished at about 10:00 PM. (It's still light here at 10:00 PM). Tomorrow morning they will all come to the flat where I'm staying for Srimad Bhagavatam class, and then we'll move on to Kursk, another city with no temple but a Nama Hatta. I'm presently moving from one city to the next, one day at a time in the Moscow region. The pace is quite exhausting, but the preaching is definitely making it all worthwhile. Mahamantra Prabhu is traveling with me right now, and he is also relishing all that is going on here. Although we don't talk about it in our lectures, nonetheless, we are still conscious of the fact that we must be prepared for whatever tests Maya throws our way. It's hard to imagine, but within a few days, it is possible that they could pass a law that would make our public preaching illegal again, and which could also restrict my entry into this country. I'm feeling the need to really impact Krsna consciousness into these people's hearts so that they can go on, under whatever circumstances will come their way. It's a great challenge, but by Lord Caitanya's mercy, anything is possible.

\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*

Thank you very much for writing something. It is a fact, the law is definitely "ugly." But we are nonetheless pushing on here. I'm getting many realizations of just how potent Srila Prabhupada's message of enlightenment to the world really is. Nothing can stop it. Not even these "ugly" laws. It's just too late. Prabhupada's books are out there. Just what shape Lord Caitanya's Sankirtan Movement will take here in the years to come is not known. But one thing is for sure. Lord Caitanya's Sankirtan Movement is here to stay. Please just bless me that I can give every breath of this life to Srila Prabhupada's mission. If wonderful Vaisnavas like your good self will give me their association here, then certainly I will consider that a great benediction. Please do come (while you can). I will be anxiously waiting.

\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*

Dear…dasa,

In principle, I am not very happy when I hear that brama- charis are engaged in business activity, and I generally never encourage it. Of course, there are bramachari disciples of mine already working for Preminvest, and I have allowed some exceptions. But, they should be known as "exceptions", and not as "principles". In general, the healthiest engagement for a bramachari is preaching and book distribution, and when not engaged in these activities, then AT LEAST, they should be under a strong bramachari leader as a spiritual authority, and should NOT be under the illusion that their "boss" at work is their "authority" for devotional service. In this particular case, the boss is a devotee, and a good devotee at that. But still, there must be more direction for a bramachari than simply what comes from the boss. If there is no such direction, then this is NOT bramachari life. Without a strong spiritual program, then the consciousness simply glides down to mundane affairs.

Considering your particular need to help your father, I can allow you to become another exception, but, only if you regularly attend either the morning program in the Moscow temple, or another morning program organized by devotees who work in Preminvest. There must be at least an arati and class. And naturally, I expect that you will chant your prescribed rounds each morning as well.

\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*

My desire is that you must accept responsibility as Acting Temple President in... Whether or not somebody replaces you in the future is in Krsna's hands. For now, you must see it as Krsna's will that, by your new responsibility, you will have the opportunity to use your inspiration for book distribution to inspire others in the temple to use their energies to support our efforts to distribute Srila Prabhupada's books locally. If we are going to distribute more books locally, then we are bound in duty to guru and Krsna to show those who will read Srila Prabhupada's books an example of what Srila Prabhupada is talking about in his books. Otherwise, we will be seen as hypocrites. Please therefore, also use your inspiration to distribute Srila Prabhupada's books. Don't lose the inspiration. Don't stay back in the temple all week long. You can go out and distribute books, at least one full day a week (and more as things improve in the temple) but for now, you must do it in...However, you must also spend time in the temple and give attention to the needs of the devotees. So many of them are feeling unsheltered as if nobody cares for their service. You must do something about this, and that takes preaching. By the time that you receive this message, dasa will have already been there, and I am sure that he has given you so much instruction. Please take his instructions seriously. I sent him there, mainly to give you support and so that you could ask him questions about how to help bring the devotees back in unity. Now act on what he has told you, and make sure that you maintain contact with him in the future. Most important of all, however, is that you should not let the pressure of the problems in Donetsk affect your sadhana. Rise early in the morning, chant your rounds attentively, without managing in the morning. Study Srila Prabhupada's books, attend Srimad Bhagavatam class and prepare for your own classes so that you can give inspiration to the devotees. And take rest at a reasonable time in the evening. You must keep a strong sadhana at all costs. If you do this, then Lord Balarama will give you the strength to carry out all of your duties.

\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*

I tried to inquire from Bhakti Vaibhava Swami myself about your situation, but for some reason, he has not been answering any of the email messages that I have sent to him for the past month. There must be something wrong with his email.

I am naturally not very happy that you are feeling so disinclined to preach in such an important city as. On the other hand, I am also aware that there have been problems there with the administration. I am not informed well enough to know whether or not the problems have improved. From your point of view, it seems that it has improved in some ways, but in other ways, it has not. Since all I have to go on is your point of view (having been unable to get a reply from Bhakti Vaibhava Maharaja), then I have to make an uninformed decision.

As you know, it is also my opinion that those who are preaching should take responsibility for those to whom they are preaching. People need to be encouraged by hearing pure, unmotivated Krsna conscious philosophy, and they will naturally gravitate towards those who preach in this way.

However, in English, there is an expression, that when people are overly zealous to deliver a message, whether it be religious or political, they stand in the street and mount on top of a "soapbox" to deliver their message to the masses. Similarly, you should be careful to not use Srimad Bhagavatam class as your "soapbox" to directly attack the policies of the administration. Naturally, this kind of preaching will not be appreciated, and, as you say, you may not be invited to give Srimad Bhagavatam class again. However, that does not mean you should stop preaching. You can deliver the same message to devotees attending Srimad Bhagavatam class without directly attacking the administration. Just present the philosophy, and use sastric examples without lodging a direct attack. You don't have to be so direct. If you are presenting the philosophy in a systematic and realized way, and if you are practicing what you are preaching, then sincere devotees will naturally gravitate towards your preaching. If you preach in this way, although your message may be very similar, then there is no need to be intimidated by the administration if they still feel threatened by your preaching. They will have to directly attack the philosophy, and you should be prepared to defend our philosophy.

That is just a principle, and I just want to caution you about not becoming overly fanatic yourself in the future. I have also seen tensions such as that which you have described in other temples, and from experience I can say that problems have also arisen from excessive fanaticism on the part of the congregational preachers as well. There are many economic hardships in Russia these days, and it is not so easy to support a "temple". Moreover, there are many devotees who have a separatist mood, and they want to control the preaching for themselves. This has also caused a lot of resistance on the part of temple administrations towards preachers who draw people away from the temple. In some cases, the congregation is used to support the material ambitions of the so- called preachers. Therefore, pure, unmotivated preaching will always bear positive fruit. If a devotee preaches without personal ambition, then wherever he may be, whether in heaven, hell, or even in or in the temple, his preaching will bear fruit. It simply takes patience, time, and faith in Lord Caitanya. He wants our preachers to become qualified in this way, but He will test our sincerity. You have already been given a prabhu-datta- desa. For now, I suggest that you just continue preaching right where you are. Remember these instructions, and then report back to me again in another month, by which time, hopefully, I can speak with Bhakti Vaibhava Swami and become better informed of what he would like you to do.

\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*

I received your letter dated September 6, and have noted the contents. Thank you very much for sending your letter and for keeping me informed.

I am happy to hear that your Nama Hatta program is continuing without interruption. Please continue to conduct these programs, and provide a facility to increase the dissemination of Krsna katha. Topics about Krsna and His pure devotees become especially potent when discussed among devotees. Moreover, kirtans should be conducted without motivation for gaining something in return. We are interested in pleasing Lord Caitanya and He is pleased by the performance of the congregational chanting of the Lord's holy names. When the chanting is performed in this consciousness, then those who are bereft of the holy name will be attracted to associate and seriously take up this chanting.

\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*

Thank you very much for sending me this report. I am always happy to hear about the preaching efforts of my disciples. When there is preaching, there is a sign of good spiritual health. I am especially pleased, considering our previous discussion in Vinnitsa, that you are keeping the determination for this important service. Please do not ever feel discouraged to preach the message of Krsna consciousness. There will always be problems in the material world, but when one distributes Krsna consciousness, one will get all the required strength to overcome all obstacles.

\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*

Dear Bhaktin,

Please accept my blessings. All glories to Srila Prabhupada.

In your November letter you've asked about your attachments to music and architecture. Of course, in Vedic culture, there is also music and architecture, but it's not the type that you'll see displayed in our present society. All music should be meant for the glorification of Lord Krsna. So if you have some attachment to music, the best thing is to dovetail it for the service of the Lord. In many temples I've heard that the matajis regularly get together in their asramas, and chant bhajans among themselves. In this way, they also learn how to use harmonium, mrdanga and kartals, and can dovetail their musical propensities for Krsna's pleasure. In our Toronto temple, there is one mataji, , who was a concert pianist, but now she plays harmonium whenever the devotees go on preaching programs, and everyone appreciates her playing very much. In this way she is satisfying her propensities for music. So maybe you can aspire to do the same.

You ask how to understand and apply the knowledge of the 10th Chapter of Bhagavad-gita. Lord Krishna is giving a detailed description of the unlimited manifestations by which He can be recognized within the material world. He then concluded a long detailed account of these manifestations by saying:

"But what need is there, Arjuna, for all this detailed knowledge? With a single fragment of Myself I pervade and support these entire universes." (Bg. [10.42](https://vedarama.com/en/books/bg/10.42))

Thus this material world is existing on one plenary portion of Krishna. If Krishna didn't enter this universe, it could not exist. In this way we must understand the greatness of God and our own identity as minute particles.

You ask how to avoid abusing one's authority or knowledge of Vaisnava rules of behavior. The best way to avoid being abusive with any authority invested to us, is to always remain humble and examplary to the teachings that we are offering to others. Otherwise, who will want to listen? We should not take advantage of authority for authority's sake. This is envy, and there's no room for envy in a spiritual movement.

And the last question from your November letter, you ask how to avoid speculating when my reply comes to your questions even though you have not asked it. Very simple. Don't speculate. Just accept it as Krsna's arrangement.

In your following letter you ask what you should write to me about. You can ask me anything that's relevant to your devotional service.

You can serve me by steadily continuing to distribute Srila Prabhupada's books, and by always thinking of ways to improve your methods of distribution. For example, I will give you some quotes from Srila Prabhupada about how he wanted his followers to distribute his books:

"If we simply speak nicely to a person and try sincerely to get him to take the book he'll take it. Why should we adopt unfair means ? We should not do anything which will create a bad impression or make us unpopular. People are after these books, they are hankering for them. We don’t need to take cheating method. I never had to use any cheating method when I first began. I simply presented the real thing. Basically and practically speaking everything that is done sincerely for Krishna's satisfaction is perfect. But we have to be very very careful in our dealings with others so that they will not take the wrong view. Just like the gopis. They left their homes in the dead of night to go and meet this young boy Krishna for kissing and embracing. To the ordinary person, this is sinful, but to the devotee, it is worshipable and it is considered the highest form of devotion even by Lord Caitanya who was himself a very strict sannyasi. Therefore we are very careful not to discuss this rasa-lila in the presence of ordinary men because they will only misunderstand and commit offence. While dealing with the ordinary men, we must spread this movement in such a way that they will not misunderstand us and take offense. Try to sell as many of my books as possible to your best ability."

"Regarding the controversy about book distribution techniques, you are right. Our occupation must be honest. Everyone should adore our members as honest. If we do something which is deteriorating to the popular sentiments of the public in favor of our movement, that is not good. Somehow or other we should not become unpopular in the public eye. These dishonest methods must be stopped. It is hampering our reputation all over the world."

My dear Tripurari das,

Please accept my blessings.

I am in due receipt of your letters dated Jan. 1st and 5th, 1975 and have noted the contents. My general request is that you all distribute as many books as possible and at the same time be very careful in dealings with others so that they may not become irritated with us. Your book sale statistics are astounding. I am so much pleased. Please continue distributing such nice quantities and try to enthuse others more and more. You are sincere, so Krishna will give you all intelligence, strength and everything else you may need to distribute millions and millions of books.

"Regarding the question you have raised about traveling sankirtana parties and selling of books, yes, we want money. So that is the real preaching, selling books. Who can speak better than the books ? At least whoever buys, he will look over.

If you have to sell books, do it by hook or by crook.

The real preaching is selling books. You should know the tactic how to sell without irritating. What your lecture will do for three minutes, but if he reads one page his life may be turned. We don't want to irritate anyone, however. If he goes away by your aggressive tactics, then you are nonsense and it is your failure. Neither you could sell a book, neither he would remain. But if he buys a book that is the real successful preaching. That is the certificate of my Guru Maharaja, if someone, brahmacari, would sell a one paise magazine, if one of our brahmacaris would go and sell a few copies, he would be very very glad and say "Oh, you are so nice!" So distribution of literature is our real preaching. Now if you cannot handle the matter nicely, that is your fault. But the success of your preaching will be substantiated by how many books are sold. Anything you want to sell, you have to a little canvass, so he gives some money for the service of Krsna. That is his good luck and he gets the chance to read some transcendental knowledge. But if you only irritate and he goes away, that is your less intelligence. The fact is that we have to adopt the same tactics as ordinary salesmen adopt, but the difference is we do it for the satisfaction of Krsna, they do it for sense gratification."

By giving you these quotes, you should not think that I'm thinking that you are guilty of improper methods of distribution. I have been giving these quotes to several of the devotees here, because it's something that I feel that all book distributors should know, and should always be conscious of. When I think of you I always think of how you are distributing Srila Prabhupada's books, and this make me very happy.

We want to continue with this book distribution on and on and on. Srila Prabhupada's vision was to change the course of this whole universe for the next 10,000 years by pushing on this book distribution. So we should always be conscious of his desires, and take up this great responsible work of inducing the people of the former Soviet Union to purchase and read Srila Prabhupada's books. This pleases me very much because it's so pleasing to Srila Prabhupada.

Since you're asking me if I'm satisfied with your service the answer is, if I told you I was, you would simply become complacent. But I'm satisfied with your sincerity. You are always anxious to please your guru and Krsna, and this pleases me very much. Please continue to meditate on your service in this way and certainly you will become successful in all of your pursuits.

No, if you feel dissatisfaction it does not mean that I am dissatisfied. It's just another one of your speculations.

Yes, austerity will help you to become humble, but it must be performed for Krsna's pleasure. Austerities alone will not necessarily help. Lord Krsna says in the Bhagavad-gita ([9.27](https://vedarama.com/en/books/bg/9.27)): "Whatever you do, whatever you eat, whatever you offer or give away, and whatever austerities you perform do that, O son of Kunti, as an offering to Me."

You write in your letter that you feel that the marathon was your happiest time and there were no problems. It's probably because you were so fully engaged and you had no time to speculate. Then every day should be just like a marathon for you. Of course, it does not mean that you have to maintain the same schedule as the Prabhupada Marathon. But each day should be so filled with devotional activities that there's just no room for maya. Wherever there is Krsna, there's no room for maya. You should be very regulated. This is an important instruction that you must follow. It will help you to control your mind.

I hope that this letter meets you in good health and enthusiastically participating in Lord Caitanya's Sankrtan Mission.

I remain, Your well-wisher, Niranjana Swami.

\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*

Dear Bhaktin,

Please accept my blessings. All glories to Srila Prabhupada.

I've read your letter dated 14.7.92, and have noted the contents.

The method which you've chosen for presenting your problems leads me to suspect that no matter what I write in this letter, you may not be satisfied. Granted, there will be some satisfaction from having at least gotten some kind of a response from me. But, it seems that you're looking for help in adjusting the external situation more than for help with your lack of internal realization. What can I do? The GBC of was just there a few days ago, and you could have written to him and sought his advice with these matters. I've never been there before, and therefore I can't tell you why your temple president treats you the way you've described. Neither can I take any action about your living arrangements. These matters must be dealt with the local authorities, and if they're not willing to help you to deal with them then you should bring them to the attention of your GBC.

As for my instructions about a particular kind of service, I would simply advise you to continue what you are already doing. But it seems that you're not satisfied with that. What's wrong with cooking, shopping, and book distribution 3-4 days a week? It sounds like an ideal engagement. It doesn't seem as though you're being forced to work long hours on sankirtan. It's a very nice combination of different services to the Deities, along with some preaching and book distribution. That's what this movement is all about!

You should learn how to become motivated to render your service, by serving the order of your guru, and not by demanding attention from your immediate authorities.
