His every feature was charming and handsome. No one was like him. Devotedly bowing down before Haridasa, the prisoners manifested all the symptoms of ecstatic love for Krsna. Seeing the advanced devotional service of the prisoners, Haridasa mercifully smiled at them. Smiling, he happily spoke this hidden benediction: 'Stay. Stay as you are. May you always be as you are now.' Not understanding the hidden meaning of these words, the prisoners became a little unhappy at heart. Then Haridasa mercifully explained the true meaning of that hidden benediction. He said, 'I have given you a blessing. But, not understanding its meaning, you are now unhappy. I did not give you a bad benediction. Please give this some thought and try to understand it. Now your hearts are attached to Lord Krsna. Please remain like that at every moment. Always gather together, chant Krsna's names, and remember Krsna. Remain like this at every moment. Never hurt anyone. Never trouble anyone. Instead, always think of Krsna and always, in a plaintive voice, call out "Krsna!" Forget about material pleasure. Flee the company of wicked men. If you stay with material pleasures you will not attain love for Lord Krsna. Stay far away from materialists. Always think of Lord Krsna. A heart attached to sense pleasures brings only trouble. Sense pleasures are a trap that catches men and women. Sense pleasures lead to destruction. By divine arrangement a fortunate person attains the association of sincere devotees. He turns from material sense pleasures and worships Lord Krsna. However, if one commits offenses, he will return to materialistic life. Please hear about this. The benediction that I spoke did not mean, "May you remain prisoners. Rather, day and night I give the benediction, 'May you forget material sense pleasures'. Playing with words, I actually gave that benediction. Please do not feel even half a sesame-seed's worth of unhappiness. I place a glance of mercy on all spirit souls. 'May you attain firm devotion to Lord Krsna', is the real benediction I give you. Please don't worry. In two or three days you will be released from prison. This I tell you. Stay in a situation of material happiness, or stay in any situation, but do not forget these truths. After giving these auspicious teachings to the prisoners, Haridasa went before the king." (end of quote)

Therefore, just as Haridasa Thakur gave the benediction to "please stay as you are", I am similarly asking you to please continue to chant under all circumstances.

It's interesting you brought up the following point, "He (Prabhupada) explains how in previous ages men had to submerge themselves in ice cold water or meditate surrounded by fire, but in this age the tapasya is not so difficult." I specifically remember Prabhupada saying exactly this in a lecture I was present at in Mayapura in 1976. It's very possible, then, that you were attending the same lecture.

Regarding your question about chanting uncovering Krsna consciousness, it is stated in Caitanya Caritamrta, "nitya siddha krsna prema sadhya kabu nay a, sravanadi suddha citte karage udoye". The translation is that "Pure love for Krsna is eternally situated in the hearts of all living beings. It does not come from any other source. But when the living entity hears about Krsna, that pure love is awakened. " Srila Prabhupada would very often quote this verse to help us understand that Krsna consciousness lies dormant in the heart of all living entities. He would give the example that just as fire lies dormant in wood, but is brought out by the catalytic contact with fire outside the wood, in the same way, Krsna consciousness lies dormant within everyone's heart. However, because Krsna consciousness has been lying dormant since time immemorial, it must be awakened by contact with Krsna. When one receives the holy name of the Lord from the right source, the pure transparent medium of the holy name, then the holy name he receives is as good as Krsna, kali-kale nama-rupe krsna-avatara/ nama haite haya sarva-jagat-nistara. "In this age of Kali, the holy name of the Lord, the Hare Krsna mahamantra, is the incarnation of Lord Krsna. Simply by chanting the holy name, one associates with the Lord directly. Anyone who does this is certainly delivered." (CC Adi [17.22](https://vedarama.com/en/books/cc-adi/17.22)) We can get the direct association with the Lord because in this age, Lord Caitanya appeared to give Himself directly, through the pure medium of His holy name. The genuine followers of Sri Caitanya Mahaprabhu, such as Srila Prabhupada, are empowered to distribute this pure holy name. kali-kalera dharma krsna-nama-sankirtana krsna-sakti vina nahe tara pravartana. "The fundamental religious system in the age of Kali is the chanting of the holy name of Krsna. Unless empowered by Krsna, one cannot propagate the sankirtana movement." (CC Antya Lila) Therefore for those who receive the pure holy name, and continue to water their devotional creeper by continuous hearing and chanting, this process awakens one's dormant love of God. Gradually the coverings that have accumulated from so many births in the material world, become cleared away.

Thank you for giving me this opportunity to associate with you. After these six weeks of hearing and chanting mundane Russian sound vibrations, this letter is a welcome relief.

\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*

In the meantime, the best way to always act is to be serving the Lord. Whether you are going to school, living in the temple, or living with family, the best way to remain active in serving the Lord is to tell everyone you meet about Krsna. This requires spiritual strength to fulfill, and to get that strength, you must strictly follow the regulative principles, and always associate with devotees for hearing and chanting about Krsna. Lord Caitanya's movement is meant for Hari-katha - not politics katha, sex-life katha, money-making katha, and so many kathas that will divert our attention from Krsna. Material consciousness means to be absorbed in all these kathas. But a devotee keeps himself under the protection of the internal potency by taking shelter of Hari- katha. That is safest way to act in all circumstances.

\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*

Regarding my instruction to remain in Dnepropetrovsk, that was always primarily based upon the fact that you had no clear alternative. How can I approve a request to "leave" one place, when I do not know where you are going? Such requests are always better when both parts of the picture are presented. If you were to ask, "Can I go to to live in the matajis asrama where dd already gave permission for me to live, and where I will be engaged in cooking for the devotees in the asrama?", then that would be clear. Or even if you asked me if you could go home and live with your family, work part- time, and visit the temple daily, concentrating on chanting your rounds, and studying Srila Prabhupada's books, then that would also be clear. It's the kind of question I could give an answer to. Not that I am saying that you should do that. But at least I could give an answer. It tells me what you are planning to do. But, "Can I leave Dnepropetrovsk?", does not tell me very much.

So my answer is, "Yes, you can leave Dnepropetrovsk, if you tell me first where you are going to go, what you are going to do once you are there, and if you are convinced that I would be satisfied that you would not be compromising your commitment to live your life as a devotee."

\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*

To become a better devotee does not necessarily mean that we must only follow the rules and regulations. That can certainly help us to become a better devotee, but it doesn't mean that we will automatically become a better devotee. For instance, just because you go to the temple on Sunday does not mean that you will automatically become a better devotee. Somebody may go to the temple, take darshan of the Deity, pray for a benediction, take prasadam, talk with friends, and then leave. They may do that every week, but that does not mean that they will automatically become better devotees. If somebody actually wants to become a better devotee, then they must also willfully cultivate the desire to do everything for Krsna. A devotee is someone who voluntarily offers his life to Krsna. That "cultivation" of the desire is what will help you to become a better devotee. Better to cultivate the sincere desire to give your life to Krsna, than to artificially renounce all your external obligations to school, family, work, etc., only to soon afterwards resume cultivating the desire for education, family, wealth, and mundane happiness. That happens when there is artificial renunciation. Real renunciation will awaken by the grace of guru and Krsna. It does not manifest immediately.

Therefore better than cultivating guilt within your heart because you are working and not regularly attending the temple, you should cultivate the desire to do your work for Krsna, and to use your education for Krsna. Instead of chanting your rounds, so that you get rounds chanted, better that you chant to cultivate remembrance of Krsna. Chanting is not a daily ritual which cumulatively brings us to love of God. Chanting in itself is an act of devotion, when we chant to petition the Lord and His internal potency "Radharani" or "Hare" to keep us under His loving protection by engaging us in devotional service. That is "cultivation." Reading Srila Prabhupada's books with the desire to associate with Krsna is again another form of "cultivation". Associating with devotees with the desire to elevate one's consciousness to remembrance of Krsna is also cultivation. These acts in and of themselves will help you to become a better devotee, because you are consciously cultivating the "desire" to become Krsna conscious. This is a far better display of devotion aimed to capture the attention of the Lord, than a forced appearance in the temple, executed without the conscious effort to remember Krsna.

Of course, by saying all this, it is not my intention to simply give you a justification for "not" going to the temple. Certainly, every visit to the temple you make, when performed in the proper consciousness will only serve to help you become a better devotee. But, the point that I am simply emphasizing is that a visit to the temple is not an end in itself. It is not the goal. Krsna is the goal (pa- sudeva para gatih), and He can be achieved only by those who cultivate Krsna consciousness under the guidance of the Lord's devotees.

Therefore, please take advantage of these points to enhance your day to day "cultivation" of Krsna consciousness, and be assured that Krsna will help you.

\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*

I will never initiate anyone who intimately associates with mystics or extra-sensory people. My experiences with these type of people are not very positive. Moreover, I have seen how they have a very negative effect on the lives of our devotees who take them too seriously. Many times I hear excuses that the devotees associate with them to preach to them, but more often than not, what is happening is that the so-called mystics are simply making new-age mystics out of the devotees. On this basis, therefore, I will not initiate anyone who intimately associates with these type of people. So, on that basis alone, I will not initiate …, and you are free to tell him that.

I will include a letter to Bhakta, which was written to one disciple of mine last year. In the later text of the letter, I refer to a person named Victor, who was also an extra-sensory person which this disciple was associating with. I believe that the instructions I gave to this disciple concerning Victor are similarly relevant to Bhakta . He can therefore refer to this text for my instruction on the matter.

\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*

Remaining a lifelong bramachari does not mean that you simply wear saffron for the rest of your life. There is a "consciousness" that must accompany life as a bramachari as well. It is foolish to think that constant meditation upon material desires will result in the deliverance of material desires, isn't it? Meditation upon devotional service, the instructions of the spiritual master, the holy name, the Lord's pastimes, will deliver one from material desire. Therefore remaining a bramachari in "THIS" consciousness will certainly lead towards relief from material hankerings, even though one may from time to time be disturbed by material desires. A bramachari will not allow himself to remain disturbed by such thoughts. By his determination for Krsna consciousness, he will push them out with a superior meditation.

This determination is bramacharya. This type of bramachari will make it in the bramacharya asrama throughout his life. A saf- fron-clad "bramachari" who hankers for the association with a woman, will simply fall into the object of his meditation.

\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*

Regarding your desire to transcribe and publish my lectures, this service would be very pleasing to me. As you know, I try to give a lot of instruction within my lectures. Having these instructions available in written form would be beneficial for my disciples. However, ideally, I would like to review the material before it gets published. I realize that this would add a new dimension to the service, one which you may not be able to execute yourself - primarily because I would have to have the transcription in English instead of Russian. However, I thought that I would ask if this is possible. If it is not, then I will agree to bypass that part of the procedure. Basically, what I would need is to have is an English transcription sent to me by E-mail of the selected lecture. After reviewing the English transcription, I would then either approve it for Russian translation and then publication, or I would recommend some editing, or in some cases I may even request it not to be published. Please let me know if this will be possible or not.

Another point is that my published lectures should be primarily for my disciples. There should be no attempt to push these publications onto other devotees. If someone wants a copy, then that is fine. But those who are not my disciples should not be canvassed to purchase a copy. So this should also be considered when considering the number of publications that would be printed. If, for some reason, publication is not possible, then having it in a format for reading on the computer is also fine.

\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*

I received your message and noted the contents. I am sorry to hear that you were unable to continue to render this service with …Prabhu. informed me a long time ago that you "disappeared" without giving any explanation. I was naturally disturbed, but trusted that you would not abandon your efforts to continue following your devotional practices.

It is nice that you are studying VTE materials, but it is better that you study "under" the guidance and discipline of senior devotees. You have a long-standing tendency to live independently, and every attempt that I've made to get you to live under an authority has failed. I agree, that to some degree, you are sincerely making an effort to live under "my" authority, but it would be better if you were guided in your studies, rather than leaving yourself open to speculation. I have heard that when you lecture, there is much speculation. So many times I have preached this point, that it is not that simply by studying books that one can become qualified. One must accept discipline and be guided. I hope that your reading will someday bring you to this understanding, and that you will voluntarily place yourself under the care of a more senior devotee.

As far as traveling with myself again is concerned, at this point, I do not see anything available within the immediate future.

Where are you living? You made no mention of that in your letter. Are you living at home again?

\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*

Therefore it seems that your awkwardness is due to the fact that you want to preach to please guru and Krsna, but you want your preaching to be more than just preaching about the activities within ISKCON. Lord Caitanya has given the order to everyone. "Just tell others about Krsna. Give the instructions given by Krsna in Bhagavad-gita and Srimad Bhagavatam." Krsna's instructions are not bound to any institution. They are transcendental to time and place. They are the Absolute Truth. If preaching about ISKCON is making you feel some lack, then you don't have to do it just for the sake of social pressure. But do not use this to justify no preaching at all. There is never any excuse for that. And neither should this be a justification to preach against ISKCON. There may be many problems in ISKCON, but it is still the institution which my spiritual master left for me to serve in, and in my humble estimation, it is still the most potent institution on the planet for carrying out Sri Caitanya Mahaprabhu's mission.

Not long ago I had written the following in a message to one godbrother:

"At this point, I am inclined to think that NM's followers should serve in their own institution, and that they should face their own problems that will inevitably have to be faced when expanding Caitanya Mahaprabhu's mission in institutional form. In that respect, I have no doubt that Srila Prabhupada's vision, expertise, and training of his followers, by far surpasses anything else on this planet. I hope that doesn't sound like a vain statement. But it's based upon my uncompromising faith in Srila Prabhupada's preaching vision."

With all of ISKCON's faults, I'm still convinced that ISKCON is being watched over by Lord Caitanya and He will protect Srila Prabhupada's sincere followers. Therefore, I remain a dutiful citizen of ISKCON, although I do not demand that my disciples must see ISKCON and Krsna as nondifferent.

For instance, read the following segment of a letter that was written last year to a disciple in the former Soviet Union.

"I can understand why you are bewildered. On one hand you are being taught a philosophy that teaches that we should learn to depend upon Krsna for everything, including our maintenance. I preach these points all the time. You surrendered yourself to the temple in that spirit, became dependent upon the temple by equating your dependence upon the temple with dependence upon Krsna. The temple encouraged that dependence by engaging you in a service, and by assuring you that you would have all that would be necessary to maintain yourself. Then all of a sudden, "Krsna" says that you are costing too much to maintain. However, that was never previously explained before in the philosophy. Now you seem to be back where you were before you knew anything about depending upon Krsna.

This is exactly one reason why I always try to emphasize the need to depend upon Krsna "beyond" the institutional dependency. Yes, it is true, that surrendering to the institution is an indication of our surrender to Krsna. Moreover, your spiritual master is also within that institution. Therefore your surrender to him, in one sense, also binds you to surrender within the institution. But if we actually want to cultivate pure Krsna consciousness we must associate with Krsna from those who are giving us Krsna. If we get Krsna's association, (Who is always beyond any institutional boundaries), then the institution doesn't become everything for us. The institution is supposed to provide the association that's required to support our Krsna consciousness. If that support is there, then we can function in any sphere of existence, whether we are being economically maintained by the institution or not. If the institution is not providing you the support that you need for your Krsna consciousness, then you must get that support from wherever you can find it. I have therefore always tried my best to give you Krsna consciousness, while I simultaneously take an active role within ISKCON. It is my duty to serve the institution of my spiritual master. I cannot whimsically abandon that duty. Therefore, I dutifully instruct my disciples to work within ISKCON, to cooperate with temple authorities, and to associate with ISKCON devotees. But beyond all that, I try to give Krsna to everyone, so that whether they are closely connected to ISKCON or not so closely connected, they will still keep connection with their spiritual master, and will hopefully appreciate that I am helping them to become Krsna conscious. " (End of quote)

So, I believe that I've said enough on that point. Regarding the points you raised about 's letter, any duty that we accept from scriptural authority, or an authority that represents Krsnas instructions to us is purifying if we execute it with Krsna's pleasure as the goal. I do not agree that unless it is applied to our psychophysical nature, it will not purify. That is not what Srila Prabhupada taught us. It is true that simply executing our duties out of social pressure will not bring about the desired result. And it is true that we find that many devotees have this experience. But whatever social duties we perform are meant to be combined with hearing and chanting about Krsna. That is what acts to purify our hearts, while we simultaneously execute our duties within society. Without Krsna katha, whatever duties we perform will not bring us to the desired goal.

This whole topic reminds me of a question one disciple asked me last week. She asked if our desires need to be fulfilled in order to advance. I answered her by telling her that all our desires must be fulfilled, otherwise whatever desire remains in the heart which is separate from Krsna will bring us back to the material world. It's true, but Krsna is the most expert in fulfilling desires. He knows how to fulfill in such a way that we won't come back and ask for their fulfillment again. The point is that although our desires do need to be fulfilled, we should let Krsna fulfill them rather than attempting to artificially fulfill them ourselves. Therefore a person is best situated for purification who depends upon Krsna for the fulfillment of all of his needs. 's point is that it's all artificial unless the full realization is there. Srila Prabhupada's point is that it is all natural if we simply strictly follow Krsna's perfect representative. Everything else is artificial if it's devoid of Krsna consciousness and that is a fact! Do you think that it's natural to relate as husband and wife in bodily consciousness? Do you think that association between male and female is natural? Natural means according to ones nature. And according to our inherent nature, we are meant to be established in a relationship with Krsna. Unfortunately,… Prabhu needed help with his own Krsna consciousness, and that is why everything seemed so artificial to him.

Given your own situation, I would simply impress on the same point. Without Krsna consciousness, then there is no doubt that the perfect execution of all of your household duties will not bring you any closer to Krsna. Therefore, within every instruction to you, I have always left you with windows to Krsna consciousness beyond the duty of mother and wife, by stressing association, reading, service to the Deities, preaching, chanting, etc. These are the duties meant to evoke Krsna consciousness, not simply being a mother and wife. If you abandon your spiritual duties, then everything else will certainly be artificial and a waste of your time. But if you take strength from your spiritual duties, then you will be able to hurdle beyond your family duties in due course of time. Krsna will fulfill all of your needs and will give you the strength to remain patient in the execution of all your prescribed duties.

In Caitanya Bhagavat there are many nice pastimes illustrating this point. When Lord Caitanya was chanting in Srivasa Panditas home, during a kirtan Srivasa Pandita's son left his body. When Srivasa Pandit saw his family members lamenting, he chastised them all, and said that they should immediately give up their false lamentation for the departure of his son. "If Lord Caitanya's ecstasy becomes disturbed by all this lamentation in this house," he said, "then I will tie a rope around my neck and drown myself in the Ganges. He was thinking only of the Lord's pleasure. Everyone entered back into the kirtan, and it wasn 't until many hours later that Lord Caitanya detected that something was wrong. When he inquired, he learned the events that had transpired, and feeling so indebted to Srivasa Pandit for his unmotivated love, he vowed that He would never leave Srivasa Pandit's association". He said, "How can I leave the company of a person, who out of love for Me, does not even know grief for his own son?" Moreover, He vowed that both He and Lord Nityananda would become the sons of Srivasa Pandit. There are many other details, but the point is that the Lord can fulfill all of one's needs if we act for His pleasure.

\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*

Although I understand why you are feeling despondent, I do not see your situation as hopeless as you have expressed it.

If you desire to live alone, and have the means to live alone, it is not prohibited. In fact, in some cases, it is better for devotees to live alone. It enables them to maintain a healthy appreciation of devotees whenever they associate.

The fact that you attend the temple programs everyday, and that it seems that you intend to continue to attend the temple programs everyday is also a very healthy sign. This means that you are taking full advantage of what the temple should offer to everyone— a place to come and hear and chant about Krsna. I am far more pleased by those devotees who live outside the temple depending upon the temple for Krsna consciousness, than those devotees who live "INSIDE" the temple but are more interested in having their bodies maintained than their Krsna consciousness. It is very disappointing when devotees live in the temple but are not interested in Krsna consciousness. This is not the reason why Srila Prabhupada established temples.

What is not clear, and this is what seems to be making you so despondent, is that you do not have any clear service connected with the temple. Please do not be so despondent about this. I have definitely acknowledged the generous donations you have provided me for my service on so many occasions. Although you may not be directly serving the temple, you have still assisted me in my preaching. For this I am grateful, and it is definitely a viable way to render service according to your means. For now, please just continue with this program of service, remain steady in practicing your sadhana, associate as much as possible with devotees, and I am confident that your sincere service will not go unnoticed by Krsna.

You have my blessings to go to the Holy Dhama this year, but please travel with other devotees and do not travel independently. Also, please take full advantage of the programs that have been arranged for all devotees attending the festival. As much as possible, you should be hearing and chanting about the Lord and His pure devotees in the association of devotees. This will certainly make your visit to the holy dhama the most rewarding for you.

\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*

Regarding your last letter, I am certainly concerned about your confusion. It seems that the temple is not providing the education and training that devotees require if they are expected to make sacrifices for their temple. I do expect more in the way of training, but I am also painfully aware of the financial struggles so many of the temples (like ) are facing. In some cases, therefore, I am recommending devotees to live outside the temple, maintain themselves independently, but they must adjust their schedules and priorities so that they can take part in the temple's devotional activities and training programs being offered at certain temples. Maybe someday… will offer such training, but it is clear that it is not offering it now. Moreover, although temple is offering training, the temple cannot provide living and maintenance for devotees attending their training programs. It is like this in many temples. At this moment, I cannot even think of a temple where I could recommend you to go.

Therefore, I strongly recommend that you complete your university education, but do it without sacrificing your Krsna consciousness. Live with other devotees, attend temple programs, and organize your schedule so that you can attend the next Bhaktin program in , which should begin in approximately three months. You should not consider it a step backward, but in fact, you should see that whatever Krsna consciousness you've gained by your surrender, should now be your foundation for moving further forward in Krsna consciousness. Lord Caitanya wants us to preach Krsna consciousness to everyone we meet. This is what protects our Krsna consciousness, no matter where we are, what we are doing, who we are surrounded by, etc., etc. You can tell others about Krsna in school. That will give you all the strength and realizations that you need to make progress in Krsna consciousness. I have no doubt that if you can master the art of being Krsna conscious while continuing your education, then Krsna will help you to find your "place" in ISKCON. ISKCON reaches much farther than life in a temple. ISKCON reaches out to everyone who takes up Srila Prabhupada's and Sri Caitanya Mahaprabhu's order to preach the glories of Sri Krsna to the forgetful conditioned souls. There is more to ISKCON than simply preaching about ISKCON. We must tell others about Krsna.

Just last week in a class that I gave in Smolensk, I was preaching very strongly that devotees do not need to package Krsna consciousness to make Krsna attractive. Krsna is already all-attractive. We may try to package Him by presenting something other than Krsna consciousness, but what is the value if people open the fancy package and cannot find Krsna? We have to depend upon Krsna, and present Him as He is. He is the Supreme Personality of Godhead. He is the Lord of all living beings. His greatest opulence is the love He displays towards His unalloyed devotees. The Lord wants to share this love with each and every one of His parts and parcels, but the forgetful living entities are turning away from Him in pursuit of worldly pleasures. We must try to turn other people's attention to Krsna. When Krsna sees us making this effort, no matter where we are, no matter what our external duties are, He is very, very pleased, and He blesses us with a mission and not only a place within ISKCON, but a place in His eternal abode. Please remember this always, and take shelter of these instructions. If you act with this faith, Krsna will always give you protection from the dangers of this material world.

\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*

No. I am not disappointed, but I am always concerned. You know how much stress I always put on the importance of cultivating Krsna consciousness. Just do not be so disillusioned to think that Krsna consciousness will automatically come. You really do have to make an effort, more than just absorbing your mind in making money and growing food. Certainly, growing food "can" be a good meditation, if it is done with the Deity's pleasure in mind. But to meditate on just growing food, can also be just another act to maintain the body. We purify all our activities for maintaining the body by our meditation on offering everything that we use in maintenance of this body FIRST for the Lord's pleasure. Then growing food, water irrigation, training bulls, and every other aspect of a simple culture becomes "Krsna" culture. Therefore please continue to make this effort to "Krsnaize" the work that you do.

I have written to dasa that I will try to schedule a visit to when I return to the United States in November. I cannot promise anything now. I will have 5-6 weeks in the U.S., and there will be many places where I will need to visit, but I will certainly try.

I was reading an article in the ISCOWP newsletter, and saw a picture of your wife with the bulls. I am sure that Balabhadra Prabhu is happy to see another future bull-worker coming up in the ranks.

Regarding my blessings for conceiving a child, you have my full blessings to conceive a nice Krsna conscious child. But please, start working to improve your Krsna consciousness now. The more Krsna conscious you are, the better chances that your child will be Krsna conscious as well. This is a very important responsibility in Krsna culture as well.

\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*

With regards to your latest letter, I will briefly answer each of your questions. However, you should know that your problems are more deep-rooted than can be dealt with by external adjustments. You really do need to develop the quality of patience, which I often describe in classes. Krsna consciousness does provide solutions to problems, but as you also have learned, Krsna consciousness is not so easily achieved. It takes patience, enthusiasm, confidence, and determination, to achieve a state of Krsna consciousness that will eradicate all reactions to past sinful life. You are still experiencing remnants from past sinful life, your lust, pride, anger, etc. All these are reactions to past sinful life. Krsna consciousness can wash all this away, but we have to be patient and prepared to go on with the pursuit of Krsna consciousness based on our faith in Lord Caitanya's mercy. A faithful devotee will go on serving despite the reactions of his past sinful life. You must be prepared to do that as well.

You are sincere, and I trust that you sincerely want to give up these sinful thoughts and desires. Krsna will help you as long as you go on struggling for Him.

Just yesterday I was writing about this point to one other disciple. Since it is relevant in my instruction to you, I will include that section of the letter here for you as well:

(Quote from letter)

"The other day we were giving a class about spiritual strength, explaining how the source of all spiritual strength is Lord Balarama. By the power of His devotional service, Lord Ananta Sesa (Balarama) holds up countless universes. He is not even aware of the universes, which rest like a single drop on one of His thousand hoods. Although He does not rest upon anything, everything rests upon Him. This is inconceivable, isn't it? How can someone not rest upon anything but at the same time unlimited universes rest upon Him? He must be the source of all strength. It is the strength of Lord Ananta's devotional service which gives Him that strength. Devotional service is the most powerful, because by the strength of devotional service, one can invoke the Supreme power from the Supreme Lord".

Similarly, in Bhagavad-gita, (3.43), Lord Krsna states, "Thus knowing oneself to be transcendental to the material senses, mind and intelligence, O mighty-armed Arjuna, one should steady the mind by deliberate spiritual intelligence \[Krsna consciousness\] and thus, by spiritual strength, conquer this insatiable enemy known as lust."

If we want the strength to overcome the fight against maya, \[lust\] then we must seek that strength from the source. There is no other way that one can be successful in the fight against the illusory energy. There may be so many diversions, distractions, and seemingly failures in the effort to fight against the illusory energy, but most important of all, is that one should never give up fighting. If we surrender to the enemy \[lust\], then, just as in war, when one side surrenders themselves to the other, they are immediately taken prisoner, their independence is lost, and they are forced to work under the jurisdiction of the enemy. Similarly, if we give up the fight against maya, then she will take us captive, and we will be forced to serve the illusory masters of lust, anger and greed with no hope for release.

But as long as one can fight, one must fight. Don't allow yourself to become so weak that you can no longer fight. The more sense gratification you engage in, the more difficult it will be to fight. But the more struggle you take for devotional service, the more you will get strength from Lord Balarama to go on fighting.

Do not become disappointed that your devotional service is not always unbroken. You will have to pass through many more tests before devotional service will come to the unbroken stage. But, as you are now experiencing, your "desire to solve and to stop this problem has become a lot STRONGER". This means that you are getting help from the source. Please always remember this, and you will be successful." (End of quote)

Now with regards to your questions:

1\) The influence of tamoguna in your life, again, the effects of past sinful life. I have given the example many times, that just as a person in a boat is influenced by the waves in the ocean, STILL, he is IN the boat. If he were in the waves, he would also be influenced, but in the middle of the ocean he would also likely drown. The person in the boat must tolerate the influence of the waves, but if he stays in the boat, he will gradually get across to the other side. Just stay in the boat! Accept that you will be influenced by the waves, but do not give up the boat of hearing and chanting about Krsna. That will protect you. "By regular attendance in classes on the Bhagavatam and by rendering of service to the pure devotee, all that is troublesome to the heart is almost completely destroyed, and loving service unto the Personality of Godhead, who is praised with transcendental songs, is established as an irrevocable fact. As soon as irrevocable loving service is established in the heart, the effects of nature's modes of passion and ignorance, such as lust, desire and hankering, disappear from the heart. Then the devotee is established in goodness, and he becomes completely happy. (SB [1.2.18-19](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-1/2.18))

2\) Thoughts of sex desire - (already answered)

3\) Pride, aggression, fear, self-defense - (already answered)

4\) Gurukula for your son - It sounds to me as though you should not bother sending your son to Mayapura. When I was in Mayapura this year, I spent a lot of time speaking with the teachers in the gurukula. They are not equipped to accept boys who are not convinced that they want to be there. Moreover, they are not equipped to accept Russian speaking boys who know no English. It has already become a problem for them. They are willing to make exceptions, if the boy is exceptional in his determination to practice Krsna consciousness, and to study, but I do not get the impression that your son is in that category. Therefore, better you just find some suitable means for him to be educated in Russia, and as much as possible, make the atmosphere in your home life Krsna conscious so that he will benefit by it.

5\) Envious, puffed-up, hanker for recognition - (already answered)

6\) Developing humility - BE HUMBLE!!! It's an order!!!

7\) Whether to leave gurukula or not - Please discuss this with… dasa. You should not leave unless there is someone suitable to replace you. And you also should not leave unless you have a suitable service to replace that service. If you are going to do Nama Hatta in …, then it would have to be connected to … dasa's preaching in the region. You should speak to him first, and ask him if he would add to his list of cities that he oversees in the region. I understand that dasa goes to once a week, but I'm not exactly sure if that is connected to dasa's preaching in the region.

8\) Boston - Presently the Boston Temple President is not accepting any more foreign devotees in Boston. Moreover, you have an eight year old son, and eight year old boys cannot live in our temples in America. It is against the law.

\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*

I received your letter dated March 4, 1997 on March 4, by E-mail, however, I did not answer it because:

1\) I was in India, attending GBC meetings at the time, and was doing a low level of correspondence while I was in India.

2\) My computer broke while I was in India.

Having returned to the United States during the earlier part of this month, I was able to get my computer repaired, and I am just now answering a lot of old correspondence.

Private instructions are those that are given through correspondence or verbally according to time, place and circumstance. Although private instruction is helpful and in some cases essential, a disciple, who nonetheless, may not have so much private instruction can still realize private instruction by strict adherence to the general instructions. This realization appears in the heart by the mercy of both the guru (externally as the spiritual master) and caitya-guru (internally, as the Supersoul within the disciple's heart). For those with firm faith in guru and Krsna, all instructions are gradually revealed in this way. However, with out firm faith in the guru's instructions, and without strict following of the guru's instructions, one cannot expect that the Supersoul will reveal everything to the disciple.

Regarding the fanatic attitude towards rules and regulations, the point is that one should not forget what is the goal of our acceptance of all rules and regulations. Rules and regulations are meant to help us become Krsna consciousness. This means that Krsna consciousness is the goal, not simply following rules and regulations. In Upadesamrta this is called niyamagraha, or following the rules and regulations for the sake of following them but not for the sake of making spiritual advancement.

Srila Prabhupada would often quote the verse, smartavyam satatam visnur, vismartavyo na jatucit....

"Krsna must be remembered at all times and He must never be forgotten. All the rules and all the prohibitions given in the scriptures are meant to be servants of these two principles". Therefore, certainly we must follow rules and regulations, at all stages of our progressive development in devotional life. But we must always remember what the purpose is behind them. "All the purposes of a small pond can be served by the great reservoirs of water. Similarly, all the purposes of the Vedas can be served to one who knows the purpose behind Them." (BG 2.46) In other words, all the purposes of following the rules and regulations given in the scripture can be fulfilled if we are conscious of the purpose behind them. "Vedesu yajnesu tapahsu caiva danesu yat punya-phalam pradistam..." "A person who accepts the path of devotional service is not bereft of the results derived from studying the Vedas, performing austere sacrifices, giving charity or pursuing philosophical and fruitive activities. Simply by performing devotional service, he attains all these, and at the end he reaches the supreme abode." (BG 8.28).

\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*

Regarding your last question about choosing a service, I have given so many instructions and so many lectures on this topic. I am surprised that you are not aware how I would answer this question. You should contact the tape ministry and ask them to send you some tapes on this subject. That would be the most effective way to get an answer to this question. However, to be brief, a "servant" doesn't choose a service according to his liking. A "servant" renders service according to the request of the master. Devotional service must come down through the authorized sources. If your authorities are telling every devotee to choose their own service, then I say that you should give this one life for distributing Krsna consciousness. That is the best way to please Lord Caitanya in this dark age of Kali.

Regarding your morning program attendance, I can accept that householders may need to conduct their own morning program at home if attending the temple program is too incompatible with their work, or, if they live too far from the temple. However, I cannot accept it, if such statements as the one that I just made are used as a justification for not attending the temple programs when it is actually possible to do so. You must be careful in this regard. As much as possible, you should attend temple services in the morning. To attend just the Sunday Feast is not enough. If you are not working seven days a week, then on those days which you do not work you should also be at the temple morning program. On the days that you do work, then your morning program at home must include at least mangala arati, japa, guru puja, and at least 30 minutes reading from Srimad Bhagavatam. This would be acceptable to me providing that all the other criteria are fulfilled as well.

In general, I am happy to hear that you have remained steady in your efforts to distribute prasadam as an honest means of livelihood. This, along with a good sadhana, is exemplary and I pray that Lord Caitanya will bless you to go on remaining a good example for others to follow.

\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*

It seems that during my visit I never answered your question regarding your singing in the chorus. As I have understood it, everything seems all right with me. Most important is that you keep yourself committed to chanting your rounds and daily absorbing Krsna consciousness philosophy. As you hopefully saw in my meeting with disciples, this, more than anything else is the primary concern for my disciples. Unfortunately, we have witnessed that from time to time, disciples "forget" that the material energy is very powerful, and that their consciousness and certain convictions that they previously held, have shifted in emphasis. As soon as we shift the emphasis from Krsna's pleasure to the bodily pleasures, we are subject to go deeper and deeper into forgetfulness of the most essential goal of our existence. Therefore, on our parts, there must be a certain level of commitment to the practices which help us remember. If we lose that sense of commitment, and illusorily think that we can just go on managing our daily affairs without having to worry about changing our consciousness to Krsna consciousness, then we will once again become covered, thinking that, "I am OK?” "This material world is not so had, after all." "Oh, all those warnings about maya are just aimed to scare us so that we can be manipulated by our authorities. There is really no need to be scared about maya". When we start thinking this way, we should know that this is the symptom that we have neglected our hearing. Therefore, I try to relentlessly hammer these points, hoping that in some of the hearts of my disciples, the message will stick, and knowing that there will always be some casualties, and therefore in the hearts of some, they will forget. Please, do not forget. If you don't forget this point, then you can assume any type of work or study for Krsna, without falling into the trap of maya. Krsna will protect you.

\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*

Hare Krsna. I meet these extrasensory people all the time in the course of my travels in the former Soviet Union. I am 100% convinced that 99.99% are complete quacks and even if they have some potency for extra sensory perception it is absolutely useless for Krsna consciousness. They are simply motivated for name, fame and a following. That is why they advertise themselves. If they were actually Krsna conscious, they would not advertise themselves nor their abilities.

Moreover, in the 11th Canto of Srimad Bhagavatam, in Krsna's instructions to Uddhava, Lord Krsna states:

"Learned experts in devotional service state that the mystic perfections of yoga that I have mentioned are actually impediments and are a waste of time for one who is practicing the supreme yoga, by which one achieves all perfection in life directly from Me."

And if you really want to expose these people, then just read to those who are being misled by these people the following crystal clear statements made directly by Srila Prabhupada from a lecture in 1968:

"Yoga practice is, therefore... It is more or less material activity because when they are powerful to show some miracles and people become captivated, "Oh, he is performing such miracle thing." In Benares in India there was a yogi. His business was anyone who will go there, he immediately produced two or four rasagullas and offer him. And many hundreds and thousands of educated men became his disciple simply for the matter, rasagulla, which is only four annas worth. So people want to see this jugglery. And those who want following or some material achievement, they want to show... Actually it is a fact. Suppose if I could manufacture rasagullas by some mantra, Hare Krsna mantra, oh, thousands of people will come immediately. You see? People want to see me, and those persons who want to have a cheap following, they want to show such jugglery. But a devotee sees... (break) ...is not of that mentality. They will simply, humble servant. They are satisfied by serving the Lord. That is devotee's position. So your statement, that "Why one should bother with these things?" That's a very nice proposal. Why? There is no necessity. Suppose if I can manufacture some rasagulla, what is the worth of this rasagulla? Oh, we can, if we spend ten cents, we can make it. So why shall I waste my energy for manufacturing rasagulla in the yoga system? Actually, therefore, Krsna says that the perfection of real perfection of yoga, the first-class yogi is he who is always thinking of Krsna. That is first-class".

Thank you very much for your letter. I can very easily forgive when I receive a letter such as that. So you would have no difficulty getting forgiveness from me. However, in Caitanya Bhagavat, when Lord Caitanya was instructing Gopal Capala about his offense to Srivasa Thakur, He stated, "When the foot is pierced by a thorn, with another thorn the first can be taken out. What will one gain by massaging the shoulders?" In other words, if you've caused a disturbance in one place of the body (the foot), then what good will it do to massage the shoulders? You must go back to the foot. You lied to the devotees in Kaliningrad and caused them inconvenience. If you want complete forgiveness, you must also apologize to them for your "pranks." Have you done that yet? After you've done that, then please send me a copy of that apology that you sent to them (specifically dasa and dasa).

\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*

When I was in in June, I had inquired about your whereabouts then. I was told that you had emigrated to …,and that's all that anyone knew. I'm grateful that you have now written to me. Thank you.

Please take it as a serious mission to associate with and assist the devotees' efforts in . Otherwise, what good can come out of emigration if you do not? Most important is that you keep association with devotees to gain strength in Krsna consciousness through hearing. If you neglect hearing, then everything else degrades from that point. From hearing will come chanting, and the strength as well to go on chanting, expanding the Lord's glories. Just two days ago, on Lord Balarama's Appearance Day here in Kiev, we were speaking in relation to this point. Lord Balarama is nondifferent than Ananta Sesa, from whose thousands of mouths are continuously coming the glories of the Lord. We were quoting Srila Vrndavana das Thakur, who offers his obeisances to Lord Balarama, whose thousand mouths are the home of Lord Krsna's glories. "As a great jewel is kept in a favorite place, so the jewel of Lord Krsna's glories is kept in the jewelry case of Lord Ananta's mouth. A person who offers prayers to Lord Balarama will find the glories of Lord Caitanya appear in His mouth." (CB Adi 11-12). We must pray to Lord Balarama, the keeper of the jewels of Lord Caitanya's glories. If we pray to Him, and hear His glories, we will get the strength to expand the Lord's glories, the service which is most dear to the Lord.

I hope that this meets you in good health, and resolute in Krsna consciousness.

\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*

… dd did make many corrections in the transcriptions that I am reading, but she did not correct all the grammatical errors. And neither am I correcting all the grammatical errors. I believe that we are both assuming that these grammatical errors and the final general editing of the text will be done by somebody who can edit the final Russian translation. It is impossible for me to correct all the errors, since there are so many errors in the use of the English words. It would take too much time for me to do this (and I believe the same goes for Parama Karuna dd). Plus, you must bear in mind that my spoken English in these lectures is also not necessarily grammatically correct, especially since I am speaking in fragments for my Russian translator. I tend to be very redundant on specific points during lectures when I need to emphasize a point, because I often feel that it's necessary to be redundant because it's expected that a little something gets lost in the immediate translation by the translator. But when the text of my lecture is written (and published), it might be better if some of this redundancy can be accurately removed by a good editor. I say "accurate", because it should be accurate. Otherwise, best to not change what I've said.

So as I once before said, I am basically just checking for philosophical correctness, and to make sure that there are not completely wrong words used which could drastically change the meaning of what I am saying.

Also, for instance, in one transcription I noticed that there were several paragraphs in the transcription where I was actually reading directly from the text in a purport from the 11th Canto of Srimad Bhagavatam, but it was transcribed as if I were speaking myself. Naturally this must be corrected, and I have noted that in the text which I will send back to you. Please take note of all the places where I have written a correction followed by \[ — -NS\]. This means that I have made an important correction that is written within the brackets. The "NS", naturally, are my initials. I will be sending you all these 23 lectures with my corrections within the next several days.

Regarding the other lectures that has, yes, they can be sent, so that I can at least print them out while I am here in Kiev. I have a laser printer that I keep in Kiev. Whether or not I will read through them all while I am here, I cannot say. But if they are printed out, there is a better chance that I will be able to gradually go through them in the coming weeks. So everything that you want me to go through should be sent to me.

I will contact Dravida Prabhu, and will ask him to send me this year's Vyasa Puja offerings, as he usually does every year, and I will send you those which you have requested. But it may take at least a week before you get them from me.

Regarding the publishing of my Caitanya Bhagavat lectures, the answer is definitely "YES". DO IT! In fact, I have been recently wondering if my disciples have been keeping a record of the Caitanya Bhagavat lectures that I have been giving. I know that I have given many, many lectures from Caitanya Bhagavat all over the CIS and in North America as well. I have been hearing from many devotees, not just my disciples, but godbrothers as well, that they are inspired by my readings from Caitanya Bhagavat. I would therefore definitely appreciate it if an EXTRA effort could be made to find as many Caitanya Bhagavat lectures as possible and to do as you have suggested. My tape ministry as well should also make a special effort to gather together these lectures. As far as CB lectures that I have given in North America are concerned, I know that I gave one in Gita Nagari, some in Boston (I remember giving one on Nityananda's Appearance Day), and I definitely gave one in Ottawa. Some guests who attended that lecture in Ottawa sent me an Email just to thank me for that particular lecture. Maybe copies of these lectures could be gathered as well.

\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*

I do not approve of separate Deity worship for those who live in a temple with presiding Deities, and as far as I know, Prabhupada did not either.

If someone were to ask me to worship their Salagrama silas, I would refuse. It is not a matter to be taken lightly. Whoever this devotee is, should have thought about things like this before he whimsically accepted the worship of Salagrama sila. The worship of Salagrama sila is not like keeping some pet, that you leave with somebody else to feed when you go on a vacation. It's a lifelong commitment, and I hope his spiritual master informed him of this when he gave him permission to worship Him.

I just traveled with Mahamantra Prabhu for 3 months, and as you probably know, he worships two Salagrama silas. He would never even think of giving his Deities over to somebody to worship. He has not missed a single day of worship in over 16 years. When he travels, They travel. He keeps Them around his neck in a bag, and takes their paraphernalia with him. I suggest that this devotee follows the same example.

Moreover, what would happen if this devotee just irresponsibly decided to stay away for more than two weeks? What would you do with the Deities then?

That is my answer.

\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*

I find nothing wrong with your desire to study English, and to live at home in order to do this, as long as by living at home you do not compromise your devotional practices. You must still rise early in the morning, eat only Krsna prasadam, chant all your rounds in the morning, and daily read from Srila Prabhupada's books. You should immediately begin reading from Srila Prabhupada's books in English, a little bit every day (if you are not already doing this). This is actually the best way to learn English. For instance, when I was studying Russian, every day I was reading and translating several paragraphs from Russian into English. This helps to build a vocabulary, especially of many of the words that we use in Krsna consciousness, and it helps to teach you proper grammar. Moreover, you are reading from Srila Prabhupada's books.

However, I would prefer if you could visit the temple for kirtan and classes more than twice a week, if it is at all possible. I am not very happy with only twice a week. Better more. Please do your best to increase it.

Please also be careful to protect yourself from nondevotional influences that come from associating with nondevotees. There is a tendency to be slack when living away from the association of devotees. However, it should be just the opposite. By practicing Krsna consciousness you should not forget that you have declared war against the illusory energy. When in maya's camp, you should be all the more vigilant to not allow her to capture you.

If you remember these points, and, above all, take every opportunity you get to tell others about the teachings of Lord Krsna...

\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*

Thank you very much for writing and giving me this report. I am happy to hear that the outcome of our meeting has led you into a position of greater stability in your service. One has to first find the position in which he can remain stable and then from that platform, one must gradually make progress. It is not good to be always unstable, sometimes in the name of surrender. In your case, if you can remain steady in all of the obligations that you've taken upon yourself, including taking care of your body, as well as if you chant your rounds attentively and hear the message of Srimad Bhagavatam in the association of devotees,

I am very hopeful that you will make progress.

Please offer my blessings to your wife, devi dasi.

I am also glad to hear that she is steadily continuing in her service as well.

\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*

Premainvest is a householder business. The owner of the business donates a major portion of his profits to ISKCON. That is his choice, and it is a wonderful choice which warrants respect. But still those who work for the business cannot be automatically called servants of ISKCON, guru, Krsna, or all three, just because they happen to work for somebody who donates his profits to ISKCON. In my estimation, the owner of Premainvest is certainly serving ISKCON very nicely. However, independently, each person who works for Premainvest must take their own initiative to offer the fruits of their work to Krsna, guru, or ISKCON (whichever the case may be) themselves. I do not encourage people to be satisfied that by working for Premainvest they are fulfilling all requirements for being engaged in Krsna's service. Premainvest is a business and should be conducted as a business. They should pay their employees, and their employees should determine for themselves how to best employ the money they make both in maintaining themselves, their families, or whatever, and in the service of the Deity, their guru, ISKCON, or ultimately all three. Beyond that, they should fulfill all requirements as ISKCON devotees, which means: chanting their prescribed number of rounds, reading Srila Prabhupada's books, attending temple programs or authorized Nama Hatta programs, and especially, facilitating the advancement of the Holy Name and Krsna consciousness, which is the primary method of sacrifice in this age.

When looking at those who work in Premainvest in this way, it could be said that anyone who works for Premainvest can also become a servant of guru and Krsna. And if you are faithfully serving my instructions as given in this letter, and as previously given in so many lectures and letters, then certainly I am pleased by your efforts. I say the word "if" because within the last year no ISKCON authority has ever given me a report of your activities. I do not accept reports from Premainvest about their employees on the same level as reports given by ISKCON authorities. They are definitely not one and the same. Therefore, please make sure that you keep yourself in the association of ISKCON devotees in the ways mentioned above, and protect your Krsna consciousness which is the most valuable asset that a living entity can obtain in this life.

\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*

Dear Bhaktin,

Please accept my blessings. All glories to Srila Prabhupada.

I have received your letter dated 10.3.92 and have happily noted the contents.

I'm happy because your letter expresses conviction in what you are doing. This means that you are hearing and understanding our philosophy. So many times I get letters, somewhat like yours, with devotees telling me that their husbands, wives, parents, etc, are against what they are doing, but they are begging me to tell them what they should do about it. In most cases, I tell them to continue to read Srila Prabhupada's books, chant Hare Krsna, and associate with devotees, and in due course of time it will become more clear what they should do. You have already come to this stage, and now you are acting with conviction. This is the best way to serve me, and is very pleasing to me.

Please maintain this conviction and go on distributing Srila Prabhupada's books with the mentality that you have, prepare prasadam and garlands for Sri Sri Gaura Nitai, and become happy in Krsna consciousness. I'm confident that Srila Prabhupada will pour his blessings upon you for your unmotivated devotional service.

I hope that this letter meets you in good health.

I am, Your well-wisher, Niranjana Swami

\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*

Dear,

Please accept my blessings. All glories to Srila Prabhupada.

First, you should know that your letter dated May 21, 1992, arrived in Boston just a few days ago. It seems that the mail has become even slower than before between Poland and the U.S.

You are right about my inability to be present in Poland (at least physically). I have no plans to go there at all this year, and at this point I don't know when I will go again. Under these circumstances, I will continue (as I always have in the past) to give you the most relevant instructions on how to remain Krsna conscious in every circumstance Krsna places you in, hoping that you will feel my presence in those instructions. That presence is far more important than my physical presence, and I assure you that you will feel my support if you make a practice of this.

You ask if you can discuss with Indradyumna Swami the details regarding your devotional service and devotional life, and at the same time continue your relationship with me. The answer to this question should be obvious. I've been telling you this all along. Whether it's Indradyumna Swami, or any other more senior Vaisnava than yourself, I've always emphasized the need for you to take guidance from someone who is more familiar with your immediate situation, as well as the situations in Poland, rather than thinking that I can just somehow, by some mystic power, adjust the circumstances for you, even though I'm not in the least bit involved with the organizational aspects of the preaching in Poland. In other words, I expect you to consult with other senior Vaisnavas, follow their recommendations, and then remember my instructions on how to execute the duties recommended for you in Krsna consciousness. This guidance from other Vaisnavas poses no threat, whatsoever, to your relationship with me as my disciple. Please always remember this.

Whether you should practice full-time devotional service, living in a temple, or should try to continue with your job, will depend upon the availablility of living facilities and support for older, more independently-minded women like yourself. In most situations I've seen that our temples lack such facilities. Under those circumstances, I feel that you should not artificially expect that all of your needs will be provided by the temple, especially considering the economic instability in countries such as yours. Of course, if you're willing to completely depend upon Krsna, and just accept what He provides you without making any kinds of demands upon the temple authorities, then it's possible. But you must be convinced that, ultimately, it's Krsna that you're depending upon, and not the temple, temple president, or even the GBC. This is Krsna consciousness. Depend upon the association of Vaisnavas for increasing your Krsna consciousness, and don't see them as instruments for fufilling your material needs. Then you may be able to practice full-time devotional service living in a temple.

But, if you can't do that, you should not foolishly think that if you live outside the temple and continue to work, that that automatically means that you are in an inferior position. That's just not true. In fact, sometimes devotees can render far more effective service in situations like that, rather than living in the temple. However, whether living within or without, devotees should live in such a way that they are in regular association with other devotees, and they are hearing about Krsna in that association.

\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*

Dear Bhakta,

Please accept my blessings. All glories to Srila Prabhupada.

I have received your letter (undated) and have noted the contents.

You ask how one can stay in this movement as long as possible, but I become a little concerned when I hear these words "as long as possible". You should be asking how one can stay in this movement, and that's it. Forget the "as long as possible". We want everyone to remain and preach in Lord Caitanya's glorious movement for the rest of their lives, right into His movement in the spiritual world. "As long as possible", suggests the potential excuse for leaving. There is no such excuse. Leaving this movement and the association of Vaisnavas is a symptom of madness, and cannot be justified by any amount of logic. In fact Lord Caitanya instructed that the most fearful situation that anyone in the universe could face is to find himself without the association of devotees. That is the answer to one part of your question.

In answer to the first part of your question, I would have to recommend Lord Caitanya's third prayer in His Siksastakam, as the best way to remain in this movement.

trnad api sunicena taror api sahisnuna amanina mananena kirtaniyah sadah harih "One can chant the holy name of the Lord in a humble state of mind, thinking oneself lower than the straw in the street, devoid of all sense of false prestige, and ready to offer all respects to others. In such a state of mind, one can chant the holy name of the Lord constantly."

One should always remember this verse and practice humility and tolerance. These are essential qualities that must be developed in order to remain in the association of Vaisnavas.

\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*

Dear Bhakta,

Please accept my blessings. All glories to Srila Prabhupada.

I have a letter dated 10.03.92 from you, but somehow, I did not receive it until my arrival in Moscow at the end of July. In it you've asked several questions which could require full lectures to give them the proper answers. However, I will answer them briefly here.

You ask how to become Krsna conscious. The answer is by pleasing guru and Krsna. Krsna is fully independent, and can act as He chooses. He does not even have to appear in our consciousness, despite our endeavors to remember Him. But if He is pleased with our endeavors to follow the orders of our spiritual master, then He becomes very inclined to reveal Himself to such a devotee. Therefore one must have the mercy of both guru and Krsna to attain the platform of Krsna consciousness.

Spiritual life is compared to a razor's edge, because maya is always waiting to capture us. Therefore at every step of the way there is danger. If one falls on a razor's edge, he may be cut very severely. Similarly, if one becomes complacent about advancing in Krsna consciousness, maya will be right there to capture us. And once maya has us, it is very difficult to break free from her clutches.

The most important principle to remember in order to perform sankirtan yajna is Lord Caitanya's third prayer in Siksastaka: trnad api sunicena taror api sahisnuna amanina mananena kirtaniyah sadah harih

"One can chant the holy name of the Lord in a humble state of mind, thinking oneself lower than the straw in the street, devoid of all sense of false prestige, and ready to offer all respects to others. In such a state of mind, one can chant the holy name of the Lord constantly."

You ask about humility and freedom from pride. In the Bhagavad-gita purport to verse 13.8-12, Srila Prabhupada writes: "Humility means that one should not be anxious to have the satisfaction of being honored by others. The material conception of life makes us very eager to receive honor from others, but from the point of view of a man in perfect knowledge—who knows that he is not this body - anything, honor or dishonor, pertaining to this body is useless. One should not be hankering after this material deception."

You ask how to realize the greatness of devotees and become their servant. One must give up all tendencies for faultfinding. Due to material contamination, the fault-finding tendency is very prominent in neophyte devotees, and therefore it is very difficult to appreciate the good qualities of the devotees with whom we must regularly associate. Don't see the faults, but learn to see the good.

The answer to your last question is to ask questions which are most relevant to your advancement in spiritual life. Because of their immediate relevance, you will naturally take shelter in the solutions given by the spiritual master. The point is, that we must clearly understand that being in this material world is like being in a blazing forest fire. When we get directions from someone how to get out, we either remember them or perish.

Hoping this meets you in good health.

Your well-wisher, Niranjana Swami

\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*

Dear Bhaktin …,

Please accept my blessings. All glories to Srila Prabhupada. I have your letter (undated) and have noted the contents. You ask me to explain what Krsna consciousness means, however, Srila Prabhupada has already explained this so perfectly throughout his teachings. Why should I even attempt to present something different? The following is the most perfect explanation from Srila Prabhupada that I've ever heard for the term Krsna consciousness:

"Krsna consciousness means to work in full knowledge of one's relationship with the Supreme Absolute and the perfection of this consciousness is full knowledge of Krsna or Supreme Personality of Godhead. A pure soul is the eternal servant of God as His fragmental part and parcel. He comes into contact with maya, illusion, due to the desire to lord it over maya. And that is the cause of his many sufferings. As long as he is in contact with matter he has to execute work in terms of material necessities. Krsna consciousness, however, brings one into spiritual life even while he is in the jurisdiction of matter, for it is an arousing of spiritual existence by practice in the material world."

It seems that your authorities are not willing to let you falsely renounce the responsibilities they've given you of taking charge of the women in the ashram. Therefore you should try to accept this duty in the true spirit of renunciation, which is to make a full endeavor in this service but always remember that the results are up to Krsna. This will help to curb the false pride that you are referring to. It seems that you're thinking that you are the more superior than others because you are their authority. But the best consciousness for one in a position of authority is to feel oneself the servant of others, and never the master. Krsna will not be so inclined to give nice results if you think that you are the controller. They may look like nice results on the external platform, but they are not, unless you're dependent upon Krsna.

\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*

October 27, 1994

Dear devi dasi,

Please accept my blessings. All glories to Srila Prabhupada.

I have your letter dated September 23, 1994, which was hand-delivered to me by dasa. Thank you very much for giving me this report about your activities. I am always happy to hear about your service in Poland, although it is not possible for me to personally be there. Nonetheless, hearing from you and devi dasi draws me to be there with you in your service.

In the Srimad Bhagavatam, first canto, chapter 13, text 43, it is stated:

"As a player sets up and disperses his playthings according to his own sweet will, so the supreme will of the Lord brings men together and separates them."

Somehow Lord Krsna has brought you together in service with devi dasi, and it's obvious that this has become a source of inspiration for you. Therefore, you should take advantage of her association, and learn to sew for Sri Sri Panca Tattva very nicely as she is teaching you. Krsna has His plan, and we must always be prepared to act as His instrument for whatever plans He makes that include us.

Lord Caitanya is very merciful, and He has expanded Himself in these five features to expand His unlimited mercy upon all the fallen conditioned souls of this age. He has therefore manifested Himself in five as a devotee, expansion of a devotee, incarnation of a devotee, pure devotee and devotional energy. And now you are Their servant.

I am also happy to hear that you are not only serving the Lord's personal form, but that you are serving His order, by going out to distribute Srila Prabhupada's books on every marathon. Please continue to fill your life with such devotional service and remain always happy in Krsna consciousness.

Hoping this meets you in good health.

Your well-wisher, Niranjana Swami

\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*

November 30, 1994

Dear dasa,

Please accept my blessings. All glories to Srila Prabhupada.

I have your letter (undated) which arrived here in Ukraine, and have noted the contents.

You ask if everything can be offered to Krsna. There are definitely some things that we simply cannot offer to Krsna. Krsna is a person, and therefore there are certain things that He will accept, and there are certain things that He will not accept. It is possible to make everything in your life offerable to Krsna, but an important point that must be understood is that until we are completely pure in heart, we are not qualified to offer anything to Krsna directly. Krsna says that one who claims to be His devotee is not His devotee. His devotee is one who claims to be the devotee of His devotee. When we offer something to Krsna's devotee, even though we may not be pure in heart, he accepts the offering, and he offers it to Krsna. But we still cannot offer anything that we want and expect the offering to be offered to Krsna. We must offer what is asked from us.

If you are reading the Bhagavad-gita, you should look in the purport of the 18th Chapter, 57th verse. There Srila Prabhupada says, "One should, however, note that, after doing something whimsically, he should not offer the result to the Supreme Lord. That sort of duty is not in the devotional service of Krsna consciousness." And he continues, "One should act according to the order of Krsna. This is a very important point. That order of Krsna comes through disciplic succession from the bonafide spiritual master."

Karmi books can be offered to Krsna if the knowledge contained within them is used in service to Krsna. If by reading karmi books, you simply forget Krsna, and do not use that knowledge for Krsna, then there is no question of being able to offer that to Krsna. That would be a perfect example of what Srila Prabhupada is referring to in the reference above.

I hope that this answers your question.

I look forward to seeing you sometime when I am back in the United States.

Your well-wisher, Niranjana Swami
