If you study Srila Prabhupada's life in the Prabhupada Lilamrta, you will see that even he did not abandon his support to his family, until he actually took sannyasa in 1958. He received the order from his spiritual master in 1922, during their first meeting. Srila Bhaktisiddhanta did not get involved with Srila Prabhupada's household affairs. He simply gave him inspiration and orders to preach the message of Caitanya Mahaprabhu.

I hope that this letter helps make things a little more clear for you.

Your well-wisher, Niranjana Swami

\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*

Dear dasi,

Please accept my blessings. All glories to Srila Prabhupada.

I received a letter from your husband about two and a half weeks ago wherein he described the problems you are facing with your pregnancy. Naturally, since having heard about this, I've been very concerned and have been thinking a lot about it. But with the schedule I'm following right now, it has been difficult for me to get the time to write a satisfactory reply.

I would imagine that in the course of trying to cope with these events, one thought that must come up from time to time is «Why is Krsna doing this to me?» Among the many other thoughts, I'm also sure that you must be thinking a lot of what potentially lies ahead when the baby is born. But you are asking for the best Krsna conscious way to focus on the situation, because without Krsna consciousness there cannot be any real peace of mind.

I'm both relieved and grateful that you have been chanting your rounds. This will undoubtedly help you to overcome the test that lies ahead for you. Just yesterday, one disciple here asked the question, «Can we advance in Krsna consciousness if we have no abilities?» I answered by emphasizing that most important is that we develop the ability to remember Krsna at all times, in all places, and under all circumstances, and quoted the verse from Srimad Bhagavatam, Second Canto, «The highest perfection of human life, achieved either by complete knowledge of matter and spirit, by practice of mystic powers, or by perfect discharge of occupational duty, is to remember the Personality of Godhead at the end of life.» And as well, in the Second Canto, Srila Sukade- va Goswami says «One who desires to become free from all miseries must hear about, glorify, and always remember, Krsna, Who is the Supersoul, and the savior from all miseries.»

In other words, when we are faced with misfortune, which comes of its own accord, we must always see it as an opportunity to increase our shelter in Krsna consciousness. Any other shelter cannot fulfill our real need. It is just an illusory conception of shelter, which temporarily gives a sense of security, but if it's contrary to Krsna's decision, it must ultimately fail. We must always be prepared for Krsna's decision. I often use the example given by Prahlada Maharaja in the 7th Canto, «My Lord Nrsimhadeva, O Supreme, because of a bodily conception of life, embodied souls neglected and not cared for by You cannot do anything for their betterment. Whatever remedies they accept, although perhaps temporarily beneficial, are certainly impermanent. For example, a father and mother cannot protect their child, a physician and medicine cannot relieve a suffering patient, and a boat on the ocean cannot protect a drowning man.» In other words, if someone is deathly sick, and his family comes into the room and says, «We have brought for you the world's best physician from the other side of the planet, and he has brought the best medicine for your disease,» the patient may immediately feel encouraged, relieved, and a greater sense of security. But is there any certainty that the patient won't die? If it's not sanctioned by Krsna, then even the best medicine and the best physician will not be able to save him. Similarly, the same can be said for the father and mother attempting to protect their child. The point is that Krsna consciousness is the only sense of reality. It is the only real security. Everything else is just a illusory conception of security and happiness, which simply cannot last. Why Krsna is doing this, we may not be able to completely understand, but ultimately must see it as an opportunity to become more attached to Krsna, our only shelter.

I'm sure that you must be aware that I often quote the verse... tat te nukampam siksamano...., «One who seeks Your compassion and thus tolerates all kinds of adverse conditions due to karma of his past deeds, who always offers his obeisances unto You, and engages in Your devotional service with his body, mind and words, is certainly a bona fide candidate of liberation.» (SB 10.14.8). This is the verse that I always remember when confronting apparent misfortune.

There is never a cause to blame Krsna for our suffering. We must see it as a result of our own sins in the past. But an expert devotee can turn any apparent reversal into something positive, if he has firm faith that, in this reversal, the Lord has a plan for his ultimate interest.

Srila Prabhupada writes in the Third Canto of Srimad Bhagavatam, Chapter 16, Verse 37: «When something is arranged by the Supreme Personality of Godhead, one should not be disturbed by it, even if it appears to be a reverse according to one's calculations. For example, sometimes we see that a powerful preacher is killed, or sometimes he is put into difficulty, just as Haridasa Thakura was. He was a great devotee who came into this material world to execute the will of the Lord by preaching the Lord's glories. But Haridasa was punished at the hands of the Kazi by being beaten in twenty-two marketplaces. Similarly, Lord Jesus Christ was crucified, and Prahlada Maharaja was put through so many tribulations. The Pandavas, who were direct friends of Krsna, lost their kingdom, their wife was insulted, and they had to undergo many severe tribulations. Seeing all these reverses affect devotees, one should not be disturbed; one should simply understand that in these matters there must be some plan of the Supreme Personality of Godhead. The Bhagavatam's conclusion is that a devotee is never disturbed by such reverses. He accepts even reverse conditions as the grace of the Lord. One who continues to serve the Lord even in reverse conditions is assured that he will go back to Godhead, back to the Vaikuntha planets.

Krsna has a plan for each and every one of us. We must simply always be prepared to continue to serve Him, glorify Him, and remember Him, while accepting His plan. If you always remember this, you will certainly be succesful in all of your endeavors. There is always success in Krsna consciousness.

I am anxious to hear whatever developments may appear. Whether externally, they appear either positive or negative, I am prepared to help you remain positive in the pursuit of your Krsna consciousness. Please keep me informed.

I am planning to return to the United States by the middle of next month. I hope to see you at that time.

Hoping this meets you in improved health.

I remain, Your well-wisher,

Niranjana Swami

\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*

Dear Bhakta, April 7, 1994

Please accept my blessings. All glories to Srila Prabhupada.

Thank you very much for your letter dated 26.02.94, which was hand-delivered to me in Mayapura. I have carefully read and have noted the contents.

I am very happy to hear that you are now chanting 16 rounds a day, but even more, that you are doing this without interfering with your studies. This requires much determination, and I ask that you please maintain it. You have my full blessings for this.

I'm also happy to hear that you like going out on sankirtan. Please continue to do this whenever it's possible.

It's nice to hear that Lord Jagannath has come to your home. I was just writing a letter to one aspiring disciple, and I was telling her how important it was to make one's home into a temple. One must rise early, offer all food before the altar, and keep very clean, both within the home as well as one's personal hygiene. These are all very important requirements. By putting the Deities in the center of the home, it helps one to always remain conscious that one is cooking, cleaning, and doing everything else in the home for Their pleasure and not for oneself. So to hear that you are cultivating this consciousness is very satisfying to me.

You are very fortunate to be learning about the Lord, and how to mold your life in service to Him. Please take advantage of all that He is giving you and make this life successful by going back home, back to Godhead.

I hope that this letter meets you in good health.

Your well-wisher, Niranjana Swami

\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*

Dear,

Please accept my blessings. All glories to Srila Prabhupada.

I have your letter which I received during my last visit to Moscow, and I have noted the contents with pleasure.

This pleasure was aroused from reading that both of you are now serving Srila Prabhupada's mission, by assisting the devotees in Moscow with the many responsible tasks that lie on their shoulders. I'm very anxious to see that the devotees living outside the temple do not simply remain as «guests», who visit the temple whenever there is a visiting sannyasi. Of course, in the beginning, that is understandable. But the more that one hears about Krsna, the more one's desire to serve Him should become aroused.

I am not introducing any new mission or exclusive services towards myself for those devotees in Moscow who are aspiring to become my disciple. The mission is already there, and it's Srila Prabhupada's mission. Anyone who takes part and assists with the activities that are already established there with faith and devotion, may offer their service to me, and I will accept it. You will help me to help my godbrothers in their service to Srila Prabhupada.

I was recently explaining this point to an initiated disciple of mine, who is now working in the BBT in Sweden. I will share some of that letter with you here, hoping that it will help to shed more light on this subject:

«For instance, Harikesa Maharaja is more directly engaged in this book publication than I am. If I assist him, it doesn't make me any less of a servant to Srila Prabhupada. This is the principle that Lord Caitanya taught us— «gopi-bhartuh pada kamalayor dasa-dasanu- dasah», that we are the servants of the servants of Krsna, the maintainer of the gopies. Therefore being the servant of the Vaisnava does not diminish our relationship with Krsna or even our spiritual master. On the contrary, it increases it. My disciples should help me serve Srila Prabhupada's mission, and that means that you can also help me serve my godbrothers who are also serving Srila Prabhupada's mission.

Another example we can use is that some of my disciples are serving in temples in the preaching zones of other godbrothers. In these zones these leading preachers are also pushing on Srila Prabhupada's mission by maintaining and opening temples, and pushing on book distribution. My disciples in these zones can serve me in assisting my godbrothers in their service to Srila Prabhupada, by working cooperatively under their authority.

So by offering your service to your spiritual master, you «are» offering it to Srila Prabhupada, and I am very eager to increase my offering to Srila Prabhupada in this way. Please continue to help me, and I'm confident that Srila Prabhupada will take notice and will pour his blessings upon you.»

So, hearing that is working in the gurukula and that is translating for the BBT is pleasing to me. Now, please continue this service with devotion, and without any expectation of return. Don't become discouraged, if your service sometimes becomes difficult. There will be so many tests. If you remain steady, despite all obstacles, continuing to chant 16 rounds daily, regularly hearing lectures from more advanced devotees, following the four regulative principles, and remaining fixed in service, you will advance in Krsna consciousness. This will satisfy me very much. I hope that this letter finds both of you in good health. Your well-wisher, Niranjana Swami

\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*

Dear Bhaktin,

Please accept my blessings. All glories to Srila Prabhupada.

I've just read through your letter completely and have carefully noted the contents.

I'm hoping that by now my letter to your husband has been translated and that he has shared the contents of that letter with you. I'm thinking that that letter may have answered practically all of the questions that you've asked me in this letter.

So, in principle, a family flat does not have to be different than a temple, as long as there is preaching. Worshipping Deities is only one aspect of Lord Caitanya's Sankirtan Movement. This is called pancaratriki-vidhi. But Srila Prabhupada always emphasized bhagavat-vidhi, or preaching the glories of the holy name of the Lord as the primary activity of this movement. Keeping the Deity in one's flat, and making Them the center of worship, purifies the householder from the reactions accumulated from the householder's need to pursue economic development. The Deity becomes the primary object of worship and not the family.

Yes, the husband has his responsibility to his family members, to see that they are becoming Krsna conscious. He must be dutiful in this regard, and not maintain neglect. However, he must also be detached from all family affairs, so that he does not lose sight of life's actual mission. In that sense, he does not center his life around his family, but rather, upon his worshipful Deity and service to his guru. He sometimes appears to create a type of conflict, since there is always the tendency to be drawn more to one side than the other. But, as the consciousness becomes more focused on Krsna, the ability to keep all of one's obligations centered upon Krsna will follow. However, in general, there will always be so many difficulties in household affairs.

Regarding the role that you must play in all of this, and your relationship with me as your spiritual master, I have already hinted upon this point in my letter to your husband. Your role is to assist him in all ways, so that he can maintain his focus upon Krsna and his spiritual master. Help him fulfill the objectives that I have laid out for him, by being a chaste and submissive wife, who does not impose unnecessary materialistic demands upon him, but instead is always there to support him in his spiritual endeavors. That is the duty of a good wife. Indirectly that is also serving me, and such good qualities in a married disciple bring me satisfaction. Of course, you also need to take every opportunity you have to preach, since that will help you to maintain the proper vision for supporting your husband. So whenever possible, you should tell others about Krsna.

I hope that these few answers, along with my letter to your husband, will help you to gain a better understanding of what is expected from you.

Hoping this meets you in good health.

Your well-wisher, Niranjana Swami

\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*

First, in response to your question about renunciation, I appreciate your ambition to remain fixed on this path. However, entering into the grihasta asrama, especially for one in a woman's body, does not have to be seen as a deviation from renunciation. There are many ways for you to remain renounced, within the protection of the grihasta asrama. «Gri- hasta» means to live together in family life for the purpose of spiritual advancement through devotional service, whereas grihamedhi means to live only for the maintenance family, and it's bodily extensions. Therefore, I expect you to remain renounced - in grihasta life.

## Preparing for death

Dear,

Please accept my blessings. All glories to Srila Prabhupada.

Part of my preparation before departure tomorrow to India, was to get an update from some devotees about your present state of health both physically and mentally. Your last letter to me seemed to indicate that your physical health is certainly not improving. Of course we all know that everything is in Krsna's hands, and if He allows you to stay in your body a long time, then no other influence will be able to stop Him. But just what Krsna's will is, is not necessarily known to us. So it seems as though you must make every effort to prepare yourself, at least mentally, for the inevitable. I'm certainly no doctor, but from the indications I've gotten from yourself and other devotees about your health, it seems as though there should be more of an effort on your part to minimize your contact with worldly affairs, and devote yourself more seriously to associating with devotees in discussions about Krsna. Lord Krsna tells us that whatever thoughts that we have at the time of giving up our body will determine our destination in the next life. It is our duty to guru and Krsna to try to make this body our last.

I got a lot of realization from this last experience with your godsister Gaurangi devi dasi from Leningrad. Her death came a lot quicker than I expected. Somehow, by Krsna's grace, I had written her a couple of letters instructing her on the importance of preparing her consciousness for death. What prompted me was that some of the devotees there indicated that she appeared to be not taking her condition very seriously. So I took it as my duty to try to make her more serious. By Krsna's grace, she took my instructions to heart, and became determined to not make her spiritual master come back to the material world on her account. The result was that so many of the devotees were thanking me for helping both themselves and Gaurangi by these instructions. I was completely overwhelmed by this reaction I got from so many of the Leningrad devotees mostly because I felt that it was not I that they should have been thanking. Those who were with her during her most crucial days became so attached to her, that it became obvious that it was Gaurangi's Krsna consciousness that made her so attractive. The instruction was there, but she made the effort. Some devotees were crying because Gaurangi helped them so much to realize the importance of becoming Krsna conscious.

So, with regards to yourself, I feel even a stronger sense of duty, having this experience with Gaurangi behind me. You say in your letter that your reading is far down. This concerns me. If anything, your reading should be far up. If you don't have the determination to read yourself then you should read with other devotees. I understand that your visits to the temple have diminished because of your present state of health. But, if you do get any opportunity to get out of your apartment to go someplace, then it seems that you should be heading directly to the temple to seek out some Krsna conscious association. This is the most important preparation for you at this time, and it seems as though it should take precedent above anything else. Please do the needful and make every effort to adjust everything accordingly.

Although I'm speaking this way, nonetheless, I obviously hope that this letter finds you at least, in improved health, if it can't be in good health.

I am always, Your well-wisher, Niranjana Swami

\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*\*

July 29, 1993 Kiev, Ukraine

Dear dasa,

Please accept my blessings. All glories to Srila Prabhupada.

I realize that now is not the time to pamper you, by trying to avoid topics related to death. Death is quickly approaching you, and because you've had the great fortune to learn how to deal with death, you know that you must make every endeavor to prepare for that moment when you pass from this mortal world. You are also very fortunate to be in the association of such wonderful devotees who know the actual goal for this human form of life. This a far superior arrangement than to be in some sterile hospital room at the time of your passing. It's no accident that you are there. Krsna has arranged this for you. Now you must engage your mind, body, and words to their full potential by discussing only topics related to Krsna. Always chant Krsna's holy name, and you will simply step on the head of death, on your way to Krsna. When Dhruva Maharaja was preparing to leave the material world, the Lord sent a flower airplane to receive him and carry him back to the spiritual world. But as he was approaching the plane, death personified appeared before him. Dhruva Maharaja simply stepped on his head, and used him as the step to enter into the flower airplane which took him to Vaikuntha. Srila Prabhupada also gives the example, that for a devotee, death and entrance into the spiritual world are just like lightning and illumination. It happens simultaneously.

There's never any fear for a devotee who engages himself fully in hearing and chanting about Krsna. There were so many comforting words spoken by Sukadeva Goswami to Maharaja Pariksit, who also was facing death within seven days. He told Pariksit Maharaja, «Both by rising and setting, the sun decreases duration of life of everyone, except one who utilizes the time by discussing topics of the all-good Personality of Godhead.» (S.B. [2.3.17](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-2/3.17)). He also told him, «O descendant of King Bharata, one who desires to be free from all miseries must hear about, glorify and also remember the Personality of Godhead, who is the Supersoul, the controller, and the savior from all miseries.» (S.B. [2.1.5](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-2/1.5))

So please take shelter of these instructions and become a glorious example for all of us, who will someday face this test as well. I will always remain, Your well-wisher, Niranjana Swami
